# ROMS 지배 방정식·좌표·시간 적분·수치 해법과 코드 대조

## 1 요약

코드 판은 `57aecf589a408b1e5490d2db7f9bd0196062a44e`이다.
`git log -1 --format=fuller`는 2026-09-18의 `Reporting Computer Memory Availability and Usage (#81)`을 표시한다.
ChangeLog의 실제 위치는 `models/ROMS/raw/source_code/roms/docs/ROMS_ChangeLog.md`이다.
첫 항목은 `docs/ROMS_ChangeLog.md:8`의 `- [2026 ROMS Log](https://github.com/myroms/roms/compare/8dc44e6...develop)`이다.
이 항목은 연간 비교 링크이다.
이 항목은 별도 릴리스 번호를 지정하지 않는다.

분류별 개수: 일치 162, A 19, B 65, C 53, D 0, E 2, F 5, H 106, X 0.
집계 근거는 RTN-001부터 RTN-412까지의 행 ID이다.
문서 대조는 340행이다.
코드 추가 처리와 결함 후보는 24행이다.
현재 노트 대조는 48행이다.

두 연직 변환식과 Vstretching=1–5의 내부 계수는 코드 계산과 대응한다.
위키의 일부 경계식에는 부호와 수평 경도 항의 차이가 있다.
위키의 tracer 이류식에는 1/8·1/6과 9/16·7/12의 차이가 있다.
위키가 제시한 Vtransform=2·Vstretching=4와 코드 초기 대입 1·1은 다르다.
입력 예제의 2·4는 입력으로 선택한 값이다.
현행 tracer 이류는 Hadvection·Vadvection의 runtime 선택이다.
현재 연직 점성·확산의 lambda 기본값은 1이다.
UV_COR 쪽의 끌어오기 원문은 UV_ADV를 가리킨다.
입력 범위 검사와 새 FB 옵션의 연결에는 코드 결함 후보가 있다.
각 결론의 원문 근거는 §2–§5의 같은 행 ID에 있다.

이 보고서는 사용자가 지정한 위키 30쪽의 텍스트를 대조한다.
위키 경로의 기준은 `models/ROMS/raw/manuals/wiki/wikitext/`이다.
코드 경로의 기준은 `models/ROMS/raw/source_code/roms/`이다.
노트 경로는 저장소 루트 기준이다.
인용의 `파일:줄` 뒤 백틱은 원문이다.
여러 줄 식은 줄바꿈을 `<br>`로 표시한다.
식의 계수와 조건은 원문을 보존한다.
표 안의 파이프 이스케이프는 Markdown 렌더링을 위한 것이다.

판독 기록은 원문 위치를 찾는 색인으로 사용했다.
판정은 `nl -ba`로 연 위키와 코드 원문에 근거한다.
이 보고서의 해석 문장은 `해석:`으로 시작한다.
`일치`는 해당 CPP 조건에서 문서의 주장과 코드 계산이 대응한다는 뜻이다.
`불일치`, `문서에만`, `코드에만` 행은 §5에서 결론을 하나씩 부여한다.
H 행의 `문서에만`은 적용 사례·성능·권고·서지·외부 도구 설명을 뜻한다.
H 행은 구현 부재를 주장하지 않는다.
모델을 실행하지 않았다.
코드 저장소의 git은 조회에만 사용했다.
노트 수정안은 제안문이다.
현재 노트는 수정하지 않았다.

## 2 대조 표

### 2.1 대상 쪽과 끌어오기

각 대상의 실질 항목과 대응 행은 아래 절에 기록한다.
`Introduction.wiki:1`은 `#REDIRECT [[Regional Ocean Modeling System (ROMS)]]`이다.
이 리다이렉트의 원 쪽은 `Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki`이다.
옵션만 끌어오는 쪽은 자체 설명 항목이 없다.
이 쪽들은 `Options.wiki`의 해당 절을 대조한다.
`NTIMES.wiki`, `TIDE_START.wiki`, `TIME_REF.wiki`는 `Variables.wiki`의 해당 절도 대조한다.
`Model_Formulation.wiki`는 목차 쪽이다.
목차의 지배식과 좌표 설명은 이 보고서의 지배식·좌표 행에서 대조한다.
위키 그림의 로컬 사본은 없다.
그림의 캡션은 텍스트 항목으로 다룬다.

개행 수와 물리행 수를 구분한다.
`wc -l`이 0인 쪽에도 마지막 개행 없는 원문 한 줄이 있다.
이 쪽의 끌어오기나 리다이렉트 원 쪽을 열었다.

| RTN-001 | 운동량 식 (1)<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:6 `\|<math display="block">\frac{\partial u}{\partial t} + \vec{v} \cdot \nabla u - fv = - \frac{\partial \phi}{\partial x} - {\partial \over \partial z} \left( \overline{u'w'} - \nu {\partial u \over \partial z} \right)+ {\cal F}_u + {\cal D}_u </math><!--\eqno{(1)}-->` | NONLINEAR && SOLVE3D. 이류는 UV_ADV. 코리올리는 UV_COR.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:580 `            ru(i,j,k,nrhs)=ru(i,j,k,nrhs)+cff1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1234 `            cff=FC(i,k)-FC(i,k-1)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1235 `            ru(i,j,k,nrhs)=ru(i,j,k,nrhs)-cff`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd31.h:282 `            ru(i,j,k,nrhs)=-0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i-1,j,k))*             &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd31.h:283 `     &                     phix(i)*on_u(i,j)` | 일치 | 코드는 u 우변에 코리올리 항을 더한다. 이류 발산과 압력 경도력에는 음의 부호를 사용한다. 연직 점성의 대응은 폐쇄식 행에 적는다. |

| RTN-013 | Reynolds flux 폐쇄 식 (7)<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:107 `To close these equations, parameterizations of the Reynolds stresses and turbulent tracer fluxes are introduced as functions of the other variables and vertical turbulent eddy viscosity and eddy diffusivity coefficients, <math>K_M</math> and <math>K_C</math>, respectively.`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:110 `\|<math display="block">\overline{u'w'} = -K_M {\partial u \over \partial z};\qquad`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:111 `\overline{v'w'} = -K_M {\partial v \over \partial z};\qquad`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:112 `\overline{C'w'} = -K_C {\partial C \over \partial z}. </math> <!--\eqno{(7)}-->`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:113 `\|(7)`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:114 `\|}`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:115 ``<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:116 `An overbar represents a time average and a prime represents a fluctuation about the mean.`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:117 ``<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:118 `Details on ROMS options for computing <math>K_M</math> and <math>K_C</math> are described in [[Vertical Mixing Parameterizations]].` | ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:923 `              FC(i,k)=cff3*cff*Akt(i,j,k,ltrc)*                         &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:924 `     &                (t(i,j,k+1,nstp,itrc)-                            &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:925 `     &                 t(i,j,k  ,nstp,itrc))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1025 `            FC(i,k)=cff3*cff*(u(i,j,k+1,nstp)-u(i,j,k,nstp))*           &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1026 `     &              (Akv(i,j,k)+Akv(i-1,j,k))` | 일치 | 코드는 평균 구배에 혼합 계수를 곱한다. 원래 방정식의 음의 flux 발산은 양의 확산 연산자가 된다. 코드는 변동량을 직접 적분하지 않는다. |
| RTN-014 | 표면 u·v 응력 조건 식 (8)<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:123 `:top (<math>z = \zeta(x,y,t))</math>:`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:126 `\|<math display="block">\begin{align}&K_m \, \frac{\partial u}{\partial z} = \tau^x_s (x,y,t) \\`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:127 `&K_m \, \frac{\partial v}{\partial z} = \tau_s^y(x,y,t) \\`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:151 `\| <math>\tau_s^x , \tau_s^y</math>`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:152 `\| surface wind stress` | !BODYFORCE.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1033 `# ifdef BODYFORCE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1034 `          FC(i,0)=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1035 `          FC(i,N(ng))=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1036 `# else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1037 `          FC(i,0)=dt(ng)*bustr(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1038 `          FC(i,N(ng))=dt(ng)*sustr(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1039 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1143 `            FC(i,N(ng))=dt(ng)*svstr(i,j)` | 일치 | 코드는 sustr와 svstr를 운동학적 응력으로 적용한다. 표면 flux는 연직 발산의 상단 값이다. |

| RTN-016 | 표면 물리 연직속도 식 (8)<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:129 `&w = {\partial \zeta \over \partial t} \end{align}</math> <!--\eqno{(8)}-->` | ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:254 `          wvel(i,j,N(ng))=pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*                              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:255 `     &                    wrk(i,j)*(z_w(i,j,N(ng))-z_w(i,j,0))+         &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:256 `     &                    cff1*(vert(i,j,N(ng))+                        &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:257 `     &                          slope*(vert(i,j,N(ng)  )-               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:258 `     &                                 vert(i,j,N(ng)-1)))+             &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:259 `     &                    cff2*vert(i,j,N(ng)  )-                       &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/wvelocity.F:260 `     &                    cff3*vert(i,j,N(ng)-1)` | 불일치 | 해석: 물질 수면에서는 w=∂tζ+u∂xζ+v∂yζ이다. 코드는 시간 변화와 수평 경도 기여를 더한다. 위키 식은 수평 경도가 0인 경우만 나타낸다. |

| RTN-018 | 저면 추적자 무flux 식 (9)<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:138 `&K_C \, \frac{\partial C}{\partial z} = 0 \\`<br>Equations_of_Motion.wiki:167 `The vertical scalar concentration fluxes may also be prescribed at the bottom, although they are usually set to zero.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:990 `            FC(i,0)=dt(ng)*btflx(i,j,itrc)` | 일치 | 무flux 식은 btflx=0인 경우에 대응한다. 위키 본문은 비영 저면 flux도 허용한다. 코드는 btflx를 적용한다. |

### 2.5 곡선 좌표와 분수 좌표

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |

| RTN-041 | 곡선 좌표의 목적<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates.wiki:3 `The requirement for a boundary-following coordinate system and for a laterally variable grid resolution can both be met (for suitably`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates.wiki:4 `smooth domains) by introducing an appropriate orthogonal coordinate transformation in the horizontal.  Let the new coordinates be <math>\xi(x,y)</math> and <math>\eta(x,y)</math> where the relationship of horizontal arc length to the differential distance is given by:`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:2 `In many applications of interest (e.g., flow adjacent to a coastal boundary), the fluid may be confined horizontally within an irregular region.  In such problems, a horizontal coordinate system which conforms to the irregular lateral boundaries is advantageous. It is often also true in many geophysical problems that the simulated flow fields have regions of enhanced structure (e.g., boundary currents or fronts) which occupy a relatively small fraction of the physical/computational domain. In these problems, added efficiency can be gained by placing more computational resolution in such regions.`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:3 ``<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:4 `The requirement for a boundary-following coordinate system and for a laterally variable grid resolution can both be met, for suitably smooth domains, by introducing an appropriate orthogonal coordinate transformation in the horizontal. Let the new coordinates be <math>\xi(x,y)</math> and <math>\eta(x,y)</math>, where the relationship of horizontal arc length (<math>dS</math>) to the differential distance is given by:` | ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:63 `!  pm         Coordinate transformation metric "m" (1/meters)          !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:64 `!               associated with the differential distances in XI.      !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:65 `!  pmon_p     Compound term, pm/pn at PSI-points.                      !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:66 `!  pmon_r     Compound term, pm/pn at RHO-points.                      !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:67 `!  pmon_u     Compound term, pm/pn at U-points.                        !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:68 `!  pmon_v     Compound term, pm/pn at V-points.                        !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:69 `!  pn         Coordinate transformation metric "n" (1/meters)          !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:70 `!               associated with the differential distances in ETA.     !` | 일치 | 코드는 수평 metric pm과 pn을 격자점마다 저장한다. 문서의 m과 n은 이 배열에 대응한다. |
| RTN-042 | 호 길이·metric 식<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates.wiki:6 `<math display="block">(ds)_{\xi} = \left( {1 \over m} \right) d \xi</math>`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates.wiki:8 `<math display="block">(ds)_{\eta} = \left( {1 \over n} \right) d \eta</math>`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates.wiki:10 `Here, <math>m(\xi,\eta)</math> and <math>n(\xi,\eta)</math> are the scale factors which relate the differential distances <math>(\Delta \xi,\Delta \eta)</math> to the actual (physical) arc lengths.`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:7 `\|<math display="block">\begin{align} (dS)_\xi &= \left( \frac{1}{m} \right) d \xi \\`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:8 `(dS)_\eta &= \left( \frac{1}{n} \right) d \eta \end{align}</math><!--\eqno{(1)}-->`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:9 `\|(1)`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:10 `\|}`<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:11 ``<br>Curvilinear_Coordinates_Transformation.wiki:12 `Here, <math>m(\xi,\eta)</math> and <math>n(\xi,\eta)</math> are the scale factors which relate the differential distances <math>(\Delta \xi,\Delta \eta)</math> to the actual (physical) arc lengths.  [[Curvilinear Coordinates]] contains the curvilinear version of several common vector quantities.` | ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:349 `          om_r(i,j)=1.0_r8/pm(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:350 `          on_r(i,j)=1.0_r8/pn(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:351 `          omn(i,j)=1.0_r8/(pm(i,j)*pn(i,j))` | 일치 | 코드는 1/pm, 1/pn과 1/(pm pn)을 계산한다. 단위는 각각 m, m, m^2이다. |

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |

| RTN-055 | 두 변환과 정의<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:2 `ROMS has a generalized vertical, terrain-following, coordinate system. | RTN-056 | Vtransform=1 식 (1)<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:10 `\|<math display="block">\begin{align}z(x,y,\sigma,t) &= S(x,y,\sigma) + \zeta(x,y,t) \left[1 + \frac{S(x,y,\sigma)}{h(x,y)}\right], \\`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:11 `        S(x,y,\sigma) &= h_c \, \sigma + \left[h(x,y) - h_c\right] \, C(\sigma) \end{align}</math><!--eqno{(1)}-->` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:158 `            cff_r=hc(ng)*(SCALARS(ng)%sc_r(k)-SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:159 `            cff_w=hc(ng)*(SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)-SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:160 `            cff1_r=SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:161 `            cff1_w=SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:162 `            DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:163 `              hwater=h(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:164 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:165 `              hwater=hwater-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:166 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:167 `              hinv=1.0_r8/hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:168 `              z_w0=cff_w+cff1_w*hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:169 `              z_w(i,j,k)=z_w0+Zt_avg1(i,j)*(1.0_r8+z_w0*hinv)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:170 `              z_r0=cff_r+cff1_r*hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:171 `              z_r(i,j,k)=z_r0+Zt_avg1(i,j)*(1.0_r8+z_r0*hinv)` | 일치 | 해석: hc(s-C)+hC=hc s+(h-hc)C이다. 위키 S는 코드 z_w0와 z_r0에 대응한다. 자유수면에는 Zt_avg1을 사용한다. |
| RTN-057 | Vtransform=2 식 (2)<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:18 `\|<math display="block">\begin{align}z(x,y,\sigma,t) &= \zeta(x,y,t) + \left[\zeta(x,y,t) + h(x,y)\right] \, S(x,y,\sigma), \\`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:19 `        S(x,y,\sigma) &= \frac{h_c \, \sigma + h(x,y)\, C(\sigma)}{h_c + h(x,y)} \end{align}</math><!--\eqno{(2)}-->` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:203 `            cff_r=hc(ng)*SCALARS(ng)%sc_r(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:204 `            cff_w=hc(ng)*SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:205 `            cff1_r=SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:206 `            cff1_w=SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:207 `            DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:208 `              hwater=h(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:209 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:210 `              hwater=hwater-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:211 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:212 `              hinv=1.0_r8/(hc(ng)+hwater)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:213 `              cff2_r=(cff_r+cff1_r*hwater)*hinv`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:214 `              cff2_w=(cff_w+cff1_w*hwater)*hinv`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:215 ``<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:216 `              z_w(i,j,k)=Zt_avg1(i,j)+(Zt_avg1(i,j)+hwater)*cff2_w`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:217 `              z_r(i,j,k)=Zt_avg1(i,j)+(Zt_avg1(i,j)+hwater)*cff2_r` | 일치 | 코드는 (hc s+hC)/(hc+h)를 계산한다. 코드는 ζ+(ζ+h)S를 계산한다. |

Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:55는 이 조건을 명시한다.<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:55 `The total thickness of the water column is <math>\zeta(i,j)+h(i,j)</math>. However, in sediment applications [[Variables#h \|h]] changes with time when [[Options#SED_MORPH \| SED_MORPH]] is activated. At input and output, the bathymetry is always a positive quantity. | RTN-059 | hc·Tcline·hmin 제약<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:29 `{{warning}} <span class="red">Warning:</span> We are now very strict about the value of &nbsp;<math>h_{c}</math>&nbsp; and other input vertical transformation parameters.  Since the beginning of ROMS,&nbsp; <math>h_c=\min(h_{min},T_{cline})</math>&nbsp; where&nbsp; <math>h_{min}\equiv\hbox{minval}(h)</math> &nbsp;and&nbsp; [[Variables#Tcline \| <math>T_{cline}</math>]]&nbsp; is the stretching parameter specified in [[roms.in]]. Notice that it is not possible to build the vertical stretching coordinates when&nbsp; <math>h_{c}> h_{min}</math>&nbsp; and using transformation [[#transform1\|(1)]] because it yields &nbsp;<math>\partial{z}/\partial{\sigma} < 0</math>&nbsp;. This has been a legacy issue in ROMS for years, and you are either consistent with the previous set-up of your application or change the value of [[Variables#Tcline \| <math>T_{cline}</math>]]&nbsp; in all input NetCDF files and [[roms.in]].  Therefore, in new applications you need to be sure that&nbsp; <math>T_{cline} \le h_{min}</math> when using the transformation [[#transform1\|(1)]].  Notice that this restriction is <span class="red">removed</span> in transformation [[#transform1\|(2)]] and &nbsp;<math>h_c</math>&nbsp; can be any positive value.  It works for both &nbsp;<math>h_c \le h_{min}</math>&nbsp; or &nbsp;<math>h_c > h_{min}</math>&nbsp;. | RTN-075 | 함수 1의 미분 가능성·표면·저면 refinement<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:107 `    <li>It is infinitely differentiable in <math>\sigma</math>.</li>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:108 `    <li>The larger the value of <math>{\theta_S}</math>, the more resolution is kept above <math>h_c</math>.</li>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:109 `    <li>For <math>\theta_B = 0</math>, the resolution all goes to the surface as <math>\theta_S</math> is increased.</li>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:110 `    <li>For <math>\theta_B = 1</math>, the resolution goes to both the surface and the bottom equally as <math>\theta_S</math> is increased.</li>` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:197 `!  If theta_b=0, the refinement is surface intensified as theta_s is`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:198 `!  increased.`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:199 `!  If theta_b=1, the refinement is both bottom ans surface intensified`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:200 `!  as theta_s is increased.`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:213 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=(1.0_dp-theta_b(ng))*                   &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:214 `     &                          cff1*SINH(theta_s(ng)*                  &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:215 `     &                                    SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k))+         &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:216 `     &                          theta_b(ng)*                            &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:217 `     &                          (cff2*TANH(theta_s(ng)*                 &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:218 `     &                                     (SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)+        &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:219 `     &                                      0.5_dp))-                   &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:220 `     &                           0.5_dp)` | 일치 | 해석: θ_B=0이면 SINH 항만 남는다. θ_B=1이면 TANH 항만 남는다. θ_S가 양수일 때 두 함수는 s에 대해 매끄럽다. |
| RTN-076 | θ_S≤8 권고·민감도<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:111 `    <li>For <math>\theta_S \neq 0</math>, there is a subtle mismatch in the discretization of the model equations, for instance in the horizontal viscosity term. | RTN-078 | 함수 2의 표면 미분 극한<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:121 `This function is similar in meaning to the [[Bibliography#SongY_1994a \| Song and Haidvogel (1994)]], but note that hyperbolic function in <math>C_{sur}</math> is <math>\cosh</math> instead of <math>\sinh</math> and <br> <math display="block"> \frac {\partial C}{\partial\sigma} \rightarrow 0 \qquad \hbox{as} \;\;\sigma \rightarrow 0.</math></li>` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:277 `            Csur=(1.0_dp-COSH(theta_s(ng)*sc_w))/                       &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:278 `     &           (COSH(theta_s(ng))-1.0_dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:279 `            IF (theta_b(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:280 `              Cbot=SINH(theta_b(ng)*(sc_w+1.0_dp))/                     &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:281 `     &             SINH(theta_b(ng))-1.0_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:282 `              Cweight=(sc_w+1.0_dp)**Aweight*                           &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:283 `     &                (1.0_dp+(Aweight/Bweight)*                        &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:284 `     &                        (1.0_dp-(sc_w+1.0_dp)**Bweight))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:285 `              SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Cweight*Csur+(1.0_dp-Cweight)*Cbot` | 일치 | 해석: COSH 기반 Csur의 표면 미분은 0이다. 가중치 μ는 표면에서 1이고 μ′는 0이다. 문서의 C′→0은 θ_S>0 분기에 대응한다. |
| RTN-079 | Vstretching=3 식 (7)·γ=3<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:125 `  <li>R. Geyer function for high bottom boundary layer resolution in relatively shallow applications, [[Variables#Vstretching\|Vstretching = 3]]. <math>C(\sigma)</math> is defined as a piecewise function:`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:127 `\|<math display="block">\begin{align}C(\sigma) &= \mu \, C_{bot}(\sigma) + (1 - \mu) \, C_{sur}(\sigma), \\ \\ C_{sur}(\sigma) &= - \frac{\log[\cosh(\gamma\,\hbox{abs}(\sigma)^{\theta_S}] }{\log[\cosh(\gamma)]}, \qquad C_{bot}(\sigma) = \frac{\log[\cosh(\gamma\,(\sigma+1)^{\theta_B}]}{\log[\cosh(\gamma)]} - 1, \\ \\ \mu &= \frac{1}{2} \left[1 - \tanh\left(\gamma\left(\sigma + \frac{1}{2}\right)\right)\right] \end{align}</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:129 `\|}where the power exponents [[Variables#theta_s\|<math>{\theta_S}</math>]] and [[Variables#theta_b\|<math>\theta_B</math>]] are the surface and bottom control parameters specified in standard input file [[roms.in]], as before. Here, <math>\gamma</math> is a scale factor for all hyperbolic functions. Currently, <math>\gamma = 3</math>. Typical values for the control parameters are:` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:357 `        exp_sur=theta_s(ng)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:358 `        exp_bot=theta_b(ng)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:359 `        Hscale=3.0_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:360 `        ds=1.0_dp/REAL(N(ng),dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:361 `!`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:362 `        SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(N(ng))=0.0_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:363 `        SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(N(ng))=0.0_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:364 `        DO k=N(ng)-1,1,-1`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:365 `          sc_w=ds*REAL(k-N(ng),dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:366 `          SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)=sc_w`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:367 `          Cbot= LOG(COSH(Hscale*(sc_w+1.0_dp)**exp_bot))/               &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:368 `     &          LOG(COSH(Hscale))-1.0_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:369 `          Csur=-LOG(COSH(Hscale*ABS(sc_w)**exp_sur))/                   &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:370 `     &          LOG(COSH(Hscale))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:371 `          Cweight=0.5_dp*(1.0_dp-TANH(Hscale*(sc_w+0.5_dp)))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:372 `          SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Cweight*Cbot+(1.0_dp-Cweight)*Csur`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:380 `          Cbot= LOG(COSH(Hscale*(sc_r+1.0_dp)**exp_bot))/               &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:381 `     &          LOG(COSH(Hscale))-1.0_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:382 `          Csur=-LOG(COSH(Hscale*ABS(sc_r)**exp_sur))/                   &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:383 `     &          LOG(COSH(Hscale))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:384 `          Cweight=0.5_dp*(1.0_dp-TANH(Hscale*(sc_r+0.5_dp)))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:385 `          SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)=Cweight*Cbot+(1.0_dp-Cweight)*Csur` | 일치 | 코드는 LOG(COSH) 표면·저면 함수를 TANH 가중치로 결합한다. Hscale=3이 문서 γ=3에 대응한다. |
| RTN-080 | 함수 3 권고값 표<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:131 `\| <math>\theta_S=0.65</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:132 `\| minimal increase of surface resolution`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:133 `\|-`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:134 `\| <math>\theta_S=1.0</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:135 `\| significant surface amplification`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:136 `\|-`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:137 `\| <math>\theta_B=0.58</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:138 `\| no bottom amplification`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:139 `\|-`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:140 `\| <math>\theta_B=1.0</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:141 `\| significant bottom amplification`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:142 `\|-`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:143 `\| <math>\theta_B=1.5</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:144 `\| good bottom resolution for gravity flows`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:145 `\|-`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:146 `\| <math>\theta_B=3.0</math>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:147 `\| super-high bottom resolution` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:341 `!        alpha : surface stretching exponent`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:342 `!                  alpha = 0.65   minimal increase of surface resolution`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:343 `!                          1.0    significant amplification`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:344 `!         beta : bottoom stretching exponent`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:345 `!                  beta  = 0.58   no amplification`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:346 `!                          1.0    significant amplification`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:347 `!                          3.0    super-high bottom resolution` | 문서에만 | 코드는 exp_sur와 exp_bot을 입력값에서 가져온다. 문서 표는 기본값 대입이 아니다. |
| RTN-081 | Vstretching=4 표면 식 (8)<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:150 `  <li>A. Shchepetkin (2010) UCLA-ROMS current function, [[Variables#Vstretching\|Vstretching = 4]].<math>C(\sigma)</math> is defined as a continuous, double stretching function: <br><br>Surface refinement function:<span id="equation8"></span>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:152 `\|<math display="block"> C(\sigma) = \frac{1 - \cosh(\theta_S \, \sigma)}{\cosh(\theta_S) - 1}, \qquad  \hbox{for }\theta_S > 0, \qquad C(\sigma) = -{\sigma}^{2}, \qquad \hbox{for }\theta_S \le 0 </math><!--\eqno{(8)}-->` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:393 `      ELSE IF (Vstretching(ng).eq.4) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:440 `          IF (theta_s(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:441 `            Csur=(1.0_dp-COSH(theta_s(ng)*sc_w))/                       &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:442 `     &           (COSH(theta_s(ng))-1.0_dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:443 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:444 `            Csur=-sc_w**2`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:445 `          END IF` | 일치 | θ_S>0이면 COSH 식을 사용한다. 그 외에는 -s^2를 사용한다. |
| RTN-082 | Vstretching=4 저면 식 (9)<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:157 `\|<math display="block"> C(\sigma) = \frac {\exp(\theta_B\,C(\sigma)) - 1} {1 - \exp(-\theta_B)}, \qquad \hbox{for }\theta_B > 0 </math><!--\eqno{(9)}-->` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:446 `          IF (theta_b(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:447 `            Cbot=(EXP(theta_b(ng)*Csur)-1.0_dp)/                        &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:448 `     &           (1.0_dp-EXP(-theta_b(ng)))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:449 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Cbot`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:450 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:451 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Csur`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:452 `          END IF` | 일치 | θ_B>0이면 표면 신장값에 EXP를 적용한다. 그 외에는 표면 신장값을 그대로 쓴다. |
| RTN-083 | 함수 4 연속성·θ 범위·rx1 권고<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:160 `The resulting stretching function is continuous with respect to <math>\theta_S</math> and <math>\theta_B</math> as their values approach zero.`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:160 `<math display="block"> 0 \le \theta_S \le 10 \qquad \hbox{and} \qquad 0 \le \theta_B \le 4 </math>` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:430 `!  This double transformation is continuous with respect to "theta_s"`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:431 `!  and "theta_b", as both values approach to zero.`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:440 `          IF (theta_s(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:441 `            Csur=(1.0_dp-COSH(theta_s(ng)*sc_w))/                       &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:442 `     &           (COSH(theta_s(ng))-1.0_dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:443 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:444 `            Csur=-sc_w**2`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:445 `          END IF`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:446 `          IF (theta_b(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:447 `            Cbot=(EXP(theta_b(ng)*Csur)-1.0_dp)/                        &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:448 `     &           (1.0_dp-EXP(-theta_b(ng)))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:449 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Cbot`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:450 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:451 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Csur`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:452 `          END IF` | 문서에만 | 해석: 함수의 0 극한은 분기값과 연결된다. 위키의 θ_B 상한은 4이다. 코드 주석의 의미 범위 상한은 3이다. 계산 분기는 이 상한을 검사하지 않는다. rx1 경고는 적용 격자의 권고이다. |
| RTN-084 | Vtransform=2·Vstretching=4 기본값 주장<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:160 `Due to its functionality and properties [[Variables#Vtransform\|Vtransform = 2]] and [[Variables#Vstretching\|Vstretching = 4]] are now the default values for ROMS.` | ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:4571 `        Vtransform(ng)=1`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:4572 `        Vstretching(ng)=1`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:982 `            CASE ('Vtransform')`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:983 `              Npts=load_i(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, Vtransform)`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:994 `            CASE ('Vstretching')`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:995 `              Npts=load_i(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, Vstretching)` | 불일치 | 위키의 기본값은 초기 대입 줄과 다르다. 기본 입력 예제의 권장 설정과 모듈 초기값을 구분해야 한다. |
| RTN-085 | Vstretching=5 분수 좌표 무번호 식<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:164 `<li>[[Bibliography#SouzaJ_2015 \| Souza <i>et al.</i> (2015)]] quadratic Legendre polynomial function, [[Variables#Vstretching\|Vstretching = 5]]. The quadratic function allows higher resolution near the surface.<br><br>The fractional stretched vertical coordinate, <math>\sigma</math>, is re-defined as:`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:166 `<math display="block">\sigma(k) = \begin{cases}-\frac{k^{2} - 2kN + k + N^{2} - N}{N^{2}-N} - \frac{1}{100} \frac{k^{2} - kN}{1 - N},        &\text{at vertical }W\hbox{-points,}  &k=0,\dots,N\hbox{,} \\ \\`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:167 `-\frac{(k-0.5)^{2} - 2(k-0.5)N + (k-0.5) + N^{2} - N}{N^{2}-N} - \frac{1}{100} \frac{(k-0.5)^{2} - (k-0.5)N}{1 - N},    &\text{at vertical }\rho\hbox{-points,} &k=1,\dots,N\end{cases}</math>` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:486 `      ELSE IF (Vstretching(ng).eq.5) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:490 `          rk=REAL(k,dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:491 `          rN=REAL(N(ng),dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:492 `          sc_w=-(rk*rk - 2.0_dp*rk*rN + rk + rN*rN - rN)/(rN*rN - rN)-  &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:493 `               0.01_dp*(rk*rk - rk*rN)/(1.0_dp - rN)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:494 `          SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)=sc_w`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:513 `          rk=REAL(k,dp)-0.5_dp`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:514 `          rN=REAL(N(ng),dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:515 `          sc_r=-(rk*rk - 2.0_dp*rk*rN + rk + rN*rN - rN)/(rN*rN - rN)-  &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:516 `               0.01_dp*(rk*rk - rk*rN)/(1.0_dp - rN)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:517 `          SCALARS(ng)%sc_r(k)=sc_r` | 일치 | 코드는 이차 다항식과 0.01 계수를 그대로 사용한다. rho 지점에서는 k 대신 k-0.5를 사용한다. |
| RTN-086 | Vstretching=5 표면 식 (10)<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:169 `As before, <math>C(\sigma)</math> is defined as a continuous, double stretching function: <br><br>Surface refinement function:<span id="equation8"></span>`<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:171 `\|<math display="block"> C(\sigma) = \frac{1 - \cosh(\theta_S \, \sigma)}{\cosh(\theta_S) - 1}, \qquad  \hbox{for }\theta_S > 0, \qquad C(\sigma) = -{\sigma}^{2}, \qquad \hbox{for }\theta_S \le 0 </math><!--\eqno{(8)}-->` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:495 `          IF (theta_s(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:496 `            Csur=(1.0_dp-COSH(theta_s(ng)*sc_w))/                       &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:497 `     &           (COSH(theta_s(ng))-1.0_dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:498 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:499 `            Csur=-sc_w**2`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:500 `          END IF` | 일치 | 함수 5의 표면 신장은 함수 4와 같다. 다른 것은 s의 이산 배치이다. |
| RTN-087 | Vstretching=5 저면 식 (11)<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:176 `\|<math display="block"> C(\sigma) = \frac {\exp(\theta_B\,C(\sigma)) - 1} {1 - \exp(-\theta_B)}, \qquad \hbox{for }\theta_B > 0 </math><!--\eqno{(9)}-->` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:501 `          IF (theta_b(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:502 `            Cbot=(EXP(theta_b(ng)*Csur)-1.0_dp)/                        &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:503 `     &           (1.0_dp-EXP(-theta_b(ng)))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:504 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Cbot`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:505 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:506 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=Csur`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:507 `          END IF` | 일치 | 함수 5도 θ_B>0에서 EXP 신장을 적용한다. |
| RTN-088 | 함수 5 연속성·θ 범위·저면 해상도 권고<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:179 `Notice that the bottom function [[#equation11\|(11)]] is the second stretching of an already stretched transform [[#equation10\|(10)]]. The resulting stretching function is continuous with respect to <math>\theta_S</math> and <math>\theta_B</math> as their values approach zero. The range of meaningful values for <math>\theta_S</math> and <math>\theta_B</math> are: <br><br> <math display="block"> 0 \le \theta_S \le 10 \qquad \hbox{and} \qquad 0 \le \theta_B \le 4 </math><br>However, users need to pay attention to the loss of resolution at the bottom.` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:518 `          IF (theta_s(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:519 `            Csur=(1.0_dp-COSH(theta_s(ng)*sc_r))/                       &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:520 `     &           (COSH(theta_s(ng))-1.0_dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:521 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:522 `            Csur=-sc_r**2`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:523 `          END IF`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:524 `          IF (theta_b(ng).gt.0.0_dp) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:525 `            Cbot=(EXP(theta_b(ng)*Csur)-1.0_dp)/                        &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:526 `     &           (1.0_dp-EXP(-theta_b(ng)))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:527 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)=Cbot`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:528 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:529 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)=Csur`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:530 `          END IF` | 문서에만 | 이 함수의 분기는 θ_S와 θ_B의 양수 여부를 검사한다. 문서의 상한과 해상도 권고를 강제하지 않는다. |

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |

| RTN-102 | 수평 orthogonal grid·metric·좌표 종류<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:4 `In the horizontal, the ROMS governing equations are discretized over a boundary-fitted, orthogonal curvilinear coordinates (<math>\xi</math>, <math>\eta</math>) grid. The general formulation of the curvilinear coordinates system allows Cartesian, polar and spherical coordinates applications. The transformation of any of these coordinates to ROMS (<math>\xi</math>, <math>\eta</math>) grid is specified in the metric terms ([[Variables#pm \|pm]], [[Variables#pn \|pn]]).` | ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:63 `!  pm         Coordinate transformation metric "m" (1/meters)          !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:64 `!               associated with the differential distances in XI.      !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:65 `!  pmon_p     Compound term, pm/pn at PSI-points.                      !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:66 `!  pmon_r     Compound term, pm/pn at RHO-points.                      !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:67 `!  pmon_u     Compound term, pm/pn at U-points.                        !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:68 `!  pmon_v     Compound term, pm/pn at V-points.                        !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:69 `!  pn         Coordinate transformation metric "n" (1/meters)          !`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:70 `!               associated with the differential distances in ETA.     !`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:349 `          om_r(i,j)=1.0_r8/pm(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:350 `          on_r(i,j)=1.0_r8/pn(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:351 `          omn(i,j)=1.0_r8/(pm(i,j)*pn(i,j))` | 일치 | pm과 pn은 실제 거리의 역수이다. Cartesian·극·구면 격자는 metric 입력으로 같은 계산 경로를 사용한다. |
| RTN-103 | Arakawa C-grid의 변수 위치<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:6 `The model state variables are staggered using an [[Bibliography#ArakawaA_1977a \|Arakawa C-grid]]. As illustrated below, the free-surface ([[Variables#zeta \|zeta]]), density ([[Variables#rho \|rho]]), and active/passive tracers ([[Variables#t \|t]]) are located at the center of the cell whereas the horizontal velocity ([[Variables#u \|u]] and [[Variables#v \|v]]) are located at the west/east and south/north edges of the cell, respectively. That is, the density is evaluated between points where the currents are evaluated.` | ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:450 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % rho(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,N(ng)) )`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:143 `            Huon(i,j,k)=0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i-1,j,k))*u(i,j,k,nrhs)*   &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:144 `     &                  on_u(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:145 `# ifdef WEC`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:146 `            Huon(i,j,k)=Huon(i,j,k)+                                    &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:147 `     &                  0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i-1,j,k))*                 &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:148 `     &                  u_stokes(i,j,k)*on_u(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:149 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:150 `          END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:151 `        END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:152 `        DO j=JstrP,JendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:153 `          DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:154 `            Hvom(i,j,k)=0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i,j-1,k))*v(i,j,k,nrhs)*   &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:155 `     &                  om_v(i,j)` | 일치 | 코드는 rho 두께를 u·v 면에 평균한다. 추적자는 셀 중심 값으로 이류 flux를 만든다. |
| RTN-104 | 동일한 배열의 수평 크기<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:12 `In ROMS all the state arrays are dimensioned the same size to facilitate parallelization. | RTN-105 | rho 범위 표<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:21 `\| <math>\rho</math>-type`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:22 `\| 1:[[Variables#Lm\|Lm(ng)]],1:[[Variables#Mm\|Mm(ng)]]`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:23 `\| 0:[[Variables#L\|L(ng)]],0:[[Variables#M\|M(ng)]]` | ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1410 `          Istr =my_Istr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1411 `          IstrP=my_Istr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1412 `          IstrR=my_Istr-1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1413 `          IstrT=IstrR`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1662 `          Jstr =my_Jstr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1663 `          JstrP=my_Jstr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1664 `          JstrR=my_Jstr-1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1665 `          JstrT=JstrR` | 일치 | 비주기 외곽의 물리 rho 격자는 내부 1:Lm,1:Mm와 경계 0:Lm+1,0:Mm+1로 대응한다. 할당 halo 범위와 구분한다. |
| RTN-106 | psi 범위 표<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:25 `\| <math>\psi</math>-type`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:26 `\| 2:[[Variables#Lm\|Lm(ng)]],2:[[Variables#Mm\|Mm(ng)]]`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:27 `\| 1:[[Variables#L\|L(ng)]],1:[[Variables#M\|M(ng)]]` | ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1411 `          IstrP=my_Istr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1663 `          JstrP=my_Jstr` | 불일치 | 문서 표는 경계 psi를 내부 범위에서 제외한다. get_bounds는 IstrP와 JstrP를 my_Istr와 my_Jstr로 설정한다. 루틴과 periodic·nesting 조건에 따라 psi 계산이 경계 또는 halo까지 포함된다. |
| RTN-107 | u 범위 표<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:29 `\| u-type`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:30 `\| 2:[[Variables#Lm\|Lm(ng)]],1:[[Variables#Mm\|Mm(ng)]]`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:31 `\| 1:[[Variables#L\|L(ng)]],0:[[Variables#M\|M(ng)]]` | ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1412 `          IstrR=my_Istr-1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1413 `          IstrT=IstrR`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1414 `          IstrU=my_Istr+1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1415 `          IstrB=IstrT+1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1383 `        IF (EWperiodic(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1384 `          Istr =my_Istr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1385 `          IstrP=my_Istr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1386 `          IstrR=my_Istr`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1387 `          IstrT=IstrR`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_bounds.F:1388 `          IstrU=my_Istr` | 불일치 | 코드는 비주기 외곽 IstrU=my_Istr+1과 주기 외곽 IstrU=my_Istr를 구분한다. 표는 이 조건을 적지 않는다. |

| RTN-111 | 수주 두께·수심 부호·SED_MORPH<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:55 `The total thickness of the water column is <math>\zeta(i,j)+h(i,j)</math>. The bathymetry ([[Variables#h \|h]]) is usually time invariant whereas the free-surface ([[Variables#zeta \|zeta]]) evolves in time. However, in sediment applications [[Variables#h \|h]] changes with time when [[Options#SED_MORPH \| SED_MORPH]] is activated. At input and output, the bathymetry is always a positive quantity. However, the depths [[Variables#z_r \|z_r(i,j,k)]] and [[Variables#z_w \|z_w(i,j,k)]] are negative for all locations below the mean sea level.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:155 `            z_w(i,j,0)=-h(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:214 `              cff2_w=(cff_w+cff1_w*hwater)*hinv`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:215 ``<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:216 `              z_w(i,j,k)=Zt_avg1(i,j)+(Zt_avg1(i,j)+hwater)*cff2_w`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:217 `              z_r(i,j,k)=Zt_avg1(i,j)+(Zt_avg1(i,j)+hwater)*cff2_r`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:218 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:219 `              z_w(i,j,k)=z_w(i,j,k)-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:220 `              z_r(i,j,k)=z_r(i,j,k)-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:221 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:222 `              Hz(i,j,k)=z_w(i,j,k)-z_w(i,j,k-1)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:207 `# if defined SEDIMENT && defined SED_MORPH`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:208 `! Since`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:76 `point '''G''' is used in this term by both points '''A''' and '''M''',`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:77 `it is not sufficient to replace its value with that of the image point`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:78 `for '''A'''.  Instead, the term <math>{\partial u \over \partial \eta}</math> is`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:79 `computed and the values at points '''D''' and '''K''' are replaced with`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:80 `the values appropriate for either free-slip or no-slip boundary`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:81 `conditions.  Likewise, the term <math>{\partial \over \partial \xi} \nu`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:82 `{\partial v \over \partial \xi}</math> in the <math>v</math> equation must be corrected`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:83 `at the mask boundaries.`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:84 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:85 `This is accomplished by having a fourth mask array defined at the <math>\psi</math> points,  in which the values are set to be no-slip in [[metrics.F]]. For no-slip boundaries, we count on the values inside the land (point '''G''') having been zeroed out.  For point '''D''', the image point at '''G''' should contain minus the value of <math>u</math> at point '''A'''.  The desired value of <math>{\partial u \over \partial \eta}</math> is therefore <math>2 u_{\bf A}</math> while instead we have simply <math>u_{\bf A}</math>. In order to achieve the correct result, we multiply by a mask which contains the value 2 at point '''D'''.  It also contains a 2 at point '''K''' so that <math>{\partial u \over \partial \eta}</math> there will acquire the desired value of <math>-2 u_{\bf M}</math>. The corner point '''F''' is set to have a value of 1.` | ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:525 `! ### 2.10 split-explicit의 단계와 판별 범위

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-117 | 시간 이력·짧은 2D 단계·정수 비율<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:112 `While time-stepping the model, we have a stored history of the model fields at time <math>n-1</math>, an estimate of the fields at the current time <math>n</math>, and we need to come up with an estimate for time <math>n+1</math>. For reasons of efficiency, we choose to use a split-explicit time-step, integrating the depth-integrated equations with a shorter time-step than the full `<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:113 `3-D equations. There is an integer ratio <math>M</math> between the time-steps. The exact details of how the time-stepping is done vary from one version of ROMS to the next, with the east coast ROMS described here being older than other branches. Still, most versions have these steps:` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:819 `            LOOP_2D : DO my_iif=1,MAXVAL(nfast)+1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:347 `        IF (iic(ng).eq.ntfirst(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:348 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:349 `        ELSE IF (iic(ng).eq.(ntfirst(ng)+1)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:350 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)*3.0_r8/2.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:351 `        ELSE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:352 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)*23.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:353 `        END IF`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:70 `      scale=(Falpha+1.0_dp)*(Falpha+Fbeta+1.0_dp)/                      &`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:71 `     &      ((Falpha+2.0_dp)*(Falpha+Fbeta+2.0_dp)*REAL(ndtfast(ng),dp))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:72 `!`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:73 `!  Find center of gravity of the primary weighting shape function and`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:74 `!  iteratively adjust "scale" to place the  centroid exactly at`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:75 `!  "ndtfast".`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:76 `!`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:77 `      gamma=Fgamma*MAX(0.0_dp, 1.0_dp-10.0_dp/REAL(ndtfast(ng),dp))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:78 `      DO iter=1,16`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:79 `        nfast(ng)=0`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:80 `        DO i=1,2*ndtfast(ng)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:81 `          cff=scale*REAL(i,dp)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_weights.F:82 `          weight(1,i,ng)=cff**Falpha-cff**(Falpha+Fbeta)-gamma*cff` | 일치 | code dt는 3D 시간간격이다. dtfast는 2D 시간간격이다. ndtfast는 그 비율을 정한다. nfast는 필터 지지 구간으로 별도 계산한다. |
| RTN-118 | 단계 1: n+1/2 tracer predictor<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:115 `#Take a predictor step for at least the 3-D tracers to time <math>n+1/2</math>.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:86 `      CALL pre_step3d (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:624 `            Gamma=0.5_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:625 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:626 `            Gamma=1.0_r8/6.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:627 `          END IF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:628 `          IF (iic(ng).eq.ntfirst(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:629 `            cff=0.5_r8*dt(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:630 `            cff1=1.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:631 `            cff2=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:632 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:633 `            cff=(1.0_r8-Gamma)*dt(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:634 `            cff1=0.5_r8+Gamma`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:635 `            cff2=0.5_r8-Gamma`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:636 `          END IF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:637 `          DO j=Jstr,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:638 `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:639 `              t(i,j,k,3,itrc)=Hz(i,j,k)*(cff1*t(i,j,k,nstp,itrc)+       &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:640 `     &                                   cff2*t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc))-      &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:641 `     &                        cff*pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*                      &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:642 `     &                        (FX(i+1,j)-FX(i,j)+                       &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:643 `     &                         FE(i,j+1)-FE(i,j))` | 일치 | pre_step3d는 중간 추적자를 t(:,:,:,3,:)에 저장한다. 시작 단계는 dt/2를 사용한다. 이후 Γ 값은 이류 방식에 따라 다르다. |
| RTN-119 | 단계 2: rho 평균의 계산 시점<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:116 `#Compute <math>\overline{\rho}</math> and <math>\rho^*</math> for use in the depth-integrated time-steps, from the density either at time <math>n</math> or time <math>n+1/2</math>.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:357 `                CALL set_massflux (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:358 `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:359 `                CALL rho_eos (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:371 `          cff1=den(i,N(ng))*Hz(i,j,N(ng))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:372 `          rhoS(i,j)=0.5_r8*cff1*Hz(i,j,N(ng))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:373 `          rhoA(i,j)=cff1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:374 `        END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:375 `        DO k=N(ng)-1,1,-1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:376 `          DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:377 `            cff1=den(i,k)*Hz(i,j,k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:378 `            rhoS(i,j)=rhoS(i,j)+Hz(i,j,k)*(rhoA(i,j)+0.5_r8*cff1)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:379 `            rhoA(i,j)=rhoA(i,j)+cff1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:380 `          END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:381 `        END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:382 `        cff2=1.0_r8/rho0`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:383 `        DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:384 `          cff1=1.0_r8/(z_w(i,j,N(ng))-z_w(i,j,0))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:385 `          rhoA(i,j)=cff2*cff1*rhoA(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rho_eos.F:386 `          rhoS(i,j)=2.0_r8*cff1*cff1*cff2*rhoS(i,j)` | 일치 | main3d는 rhs3d의 predictor 전에 rho_eos를 호출한다. 따라서 문서가 허용한 시간 n의 density 경로에 대응한다. |
| RTN-120 | 단계 3: 3D 우변의 수직 적분·외삽<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:117 `#Depth integrate the 3-D momentum right-hand side terms at time <math>n+1/2</math> for use in the depth-integrated time-steps (or extrapolate to obtain an estimate of those terms).` | ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1819 `            rufrc(i,j)=rufrc(i,j)+ru(i,j,k,nrhs)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2416 `          cff1=23.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2417 `          cff2=16.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2418 `          cff3= 5.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2419 `          DO j=Jstr,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2420 `            DO i=IstrU,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2421 `              rufrc(i,j)=rufrc(i,j)-rhs_ubar(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2422 `              rhs_ubar(i,j)=rhs_ubar(i,j)+                              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2423 `     &                      cff1*rufrc(i,j)-                            &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2424 `     &                      cff2*ru(i,j,0,nnew)+                        &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2425 `     &                      cff3*ru(i,j,0,nstp)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2426 `              ru(i,j,0,nstp)=rufrc(i,j)` | 일치 | 코드는 층 적분 우변을 합한다. 2D 우변을 빼고 저장한 slow forcing을 AB3 계수로 결합한다. |
| RTN-121 | 단계 4: 2D 적분·두 시간 평균<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:118 `#Take all the depth-integrated steps. Store weighted time-means of the <math>\overline{u}</math>, <math>\overline{v}</math> fields centered at both time <math>n+1/2</math> and time <math>n+1</math> (plus <math>\zeta</math> at time <math>n+1</math>). recursive`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1350 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1351 `# if defined STEP2D_FB_AB3_AM4 \|\| defined STEP2D_FB_LF_AM3`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1352 `          ubar(i,j,knew)=DC(i,0)*DU_avg1(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1353 `#  ifdef WET_DRY`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1354 `          ubar(i,j,knew)=ubar(i,j,knew)*umask_wet(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1355 `#  endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1356 `# else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:1357 `          ubar(i,j,1)=DC(i,0)*DU_avg1(i,j)` | 불일치 | 코드는 시간 평균한 수송량 DU_avg1·DU_avg2·DV_avg1·DV_avg2를 저장한다. 위키는 ubar·vbar의 weighted time-mean이라고 적는다. 위키는 수송량 평균을 수심·면 길이로 나눠 속도를 복원하는 계산을 적지 않는다. 해석: 변하는 수심 D에서 평균(D*ubar)를 평균(D)로 나눈 값은 동일한 시간 가중치로 계산한 평균(ubar)와 일반적으로 다르다. |
| RTN-122 | 단계 5: 평균으로 3D corrector 완성<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:119 `#Use the weighted time-means from depth-integrated fields to complete the corrector step for the 3-D fields to time <math>n+1</math>.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:999 `                CALL step3d_uv (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:694 `          cff1=1.0_r8/(CF(i,0)*on_u(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:695 `          DC(i,0)=(DC(i,0)*on_u(i,j)-DU_avg1(i,j))*cff1      ! recursive`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:715 `            u(i,j,k,nnew)=u(i,j,k,nnew)-DC(i,0)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1067 `                CALL step3d_t (ng, tile)` | 일치 | 코드는 3D 운동량의 기존 수직 평균을 제거하고 DU_avg1으로 맞춘다. 그 뒤 tracer를 갱신한다. |
| RTN-123 | mode-splitting instability 회피·계열별 그림<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:120 `Great care is taken to avoid the introduction of a mode-splitting`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:121 `instability due to the use of shorter time steps for the depth-integrated`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:122 `computations.`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:123 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:124 `The mode coupling has evolved through the various ROMS versions, as shown in the figures below [from [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2008a \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams (2008a)]]]. The time-stepping schemes are also listed in the following table and described in detail in [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2005a \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams (2005)]] and [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2008b \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams (2009)]]; the relevant ones are described in [[Time-stepping Schemes Review]].`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:125 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:126 `The following figures show the time-stepping and mode coupling used in the various ROMS versions. In all, the curved arrows update the 3-D fields; those with "pillars" are leapfrog in nature with the pillar representing the r.h.s. terms. Straight arrows indicate exchange between the barotropic and baroclinic modes. The shape functions for the fast time steps show just one option out of many possibilities. The grey function has weights to produce an estimate at time <math>n+1</math>, while the light red function has weights to produce an estimate at time <math>n+1/2</math>.`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:127 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:128 `Rutgers ROMS:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:129 `[[Image:Timestep_1.png]]`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:130 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:131 `ROMS AGRIF:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:132 `[[Image:Timestep_2.png]]`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:133 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:134 `UCLA ROMS:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:135 `[[Image:Timestep_3.png]]`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:136 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:137 `Non-hydrostatic ROMS:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:138 `[[Image:Timestep_4.png]]` | 계산 대응 없음. | 문서에만 | 이 문단은 모델 계열별 설명이다. 로컬 그림이 없다. 비정수압 계열은 이번 Rutgers 정수압 코드의 계산 대상이 아니다. |

FB`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:160 `(AB3-AM4)` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d.F:20 `# include "step2d_LF_AM3.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:813 `!  Solve the vertically integrated primitive equations for the`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:814 `!  free-surface and barotropic momentum components using a predictor-`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:815 `!  corrector LeapFrog with 3rd-order Adams-Moulton (LF-AM3) time`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:816 `!  stepping scheme.` | 일치 | 표의 Rutgers LF-AM3는 현행 기본 include와 대응한다. 나머지 열은 다른 계열의 역사 설명이다. 추가 FB 옵션의 코드 연결 문제는 §3에서 판정한다. |
| RTN-125 | 시간 적분 표: 2D αmax·반복 수<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:162 `\| 2-D <math>\alpha_{max}</math>, iter.`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:163 `\| <math>\sqrt{2}</math>, (2)<math>\ddagger</math>`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:164 `\| 1.85, (2)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:165 `\| 1.85, (2)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:166 `\| 1.78, (1)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:167 `\| 1.78, (1)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:219 `<math>\dagger</math>The generalized FB barotropic mode was ported into the newest AGRIF code at the end of 2007.`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:220 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:221 `<math>\ddagger</math>The number in parentheses (e.g., 2) indicates the number of r.h.s. computations per time step. As a consequence of eq. (1), if the tracer is spatially uniform, it will remain so regardless of the velocity field (constancy preservation). On the other hand, as a consequence of (2), the volume integral of the tracer concentration is conserved in the absence of internal sources and fluxes through the boundary. | RTN-143 | sqrt(gh)·split timestep·그림<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:367 `The presence of a free surface introduces waves which propagate at a speed of <math>\sqrt{gh}</math>.  These waves usually impose a more severe time-step limit than any of the internal processes.  We have therefore chosen to solve the full equations by means of a split time step.  In other words, the depth integrated equations (9), (10), and (6) are integrated using a short time step and the values of <math>\overline{u}</math> and <math>\overline{v}</math> are used to replace those found by integrating the full equations on a longer time step.  A diagram of the barotropic time stepping is shown here:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:368 `[[Image:Shortstep.png]]` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:819 `            LOOP_2D : DO my_iif=1,MAXVAL(nfast)+1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:694 `          cff1=1.0_r8/(CF(i,0)*on_u(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:695 `          DC(i,0)=(DC(i,0)*on_u(i,j)-DU_avg1(i,j))*cff1      ! recursive` | 문서에만 | 파속과 효율 설명은 이론·성능 주장이다. split-explicit과 평균 교체의 구현은 별도 행에서 확인했다. 그림은 없다. |

| RTN-146 | slow forcing 차분 절차<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:414 `When time stepping the model, we compute the right-hand-sides for the full 3-D momentum equations as well as the right-hand-sides for equations (11) and (12).  The vertical integral of the 3-D right-hand-sides are obtained and then the 2-D right-hand-sides are subtracted.  The resulting fields are the slow forcings <math>R_{u_{\rm slow}}</math> and <math>R_{v_{\rm slow}}</math>.  This was found to be the easiest way to retain the baroclinic contributions of the non-linear terms such as <math>\overline{uu} - \overline{u}\,\overline{u}</math>.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2302 `! "rhs_ubar" and "rhs_vbar" during all subsequent time steps.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2305 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2306 `      IF (FIRST_2D_STEP.and.PREDICTOR_2D_STEP(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2307 `        IF (iic(ng).eq.ntfirst(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2308 `          DO j=Jstr,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2309 `            DO i=IstrU,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2310 `              rufrc(i,j)=rufrc(i,j)-rhs_ubar(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2311 `              rhs_ubar(i,j)=rhs_ubar(i,j)+rufrc(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2312 `              ru(i,j,0,nstp)=rufrc(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2421 `              rufrc(i,j)=rufrc(i,j)-rhs_ubar(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2422 `              rhs_ubar(i,j)=rhs_ubar(i,j)+                              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2423 `     &                      cff1*rufrc(i,j)-                            &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2424 `     &                      cff2*ru(i,j,0,nnew)+                        &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2425 `     &                      cff3*ru(i,j,0,nstp)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2426 `              ru(i,j,0,nstp)=rufrc(i,j)` | 일치 | 코드는 최초 predictor에서 2D 우변을 한 번 뺀다. 3D 비선형 항과 2D 평균 운동의 차가 slow forcing에 남는다. |

| RTN-147 | 연속식 선행·predictor/corrector·Mstar·수심 갱신<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:416 `The model is time stepped from time <math>n</math> to time <math>n+1</math> by using short time steps on equations (11), (12) and (6). Equation (6) is time stepped first, so that an estimate of the new <math>D</math> is available for the time rate of change terms in equations (11) and (12). A third-order predictor-corrector time stepping is used. In practice, we actually time step all the way to time <math>(n+</math> '''dtfast''' <math>\times M^\star)</math> and while maintaining weighted averages of the values of <math>\overline{u}</math>, <math>\overline{v}</math> and <math>\zeta</math>.  The averages are used to replace the values at time <math>n+1</math> in both the baroclinic and barotropic modes, and for recomputing the vertical grid spacing <math>H_z</math>. | 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |

The pressure gradient therefore has the form:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:473 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:474 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:475 `\|<math display="block"> -\frac{g D}{n} \frac{\partial \zeta}{\partial \xi} +`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:476 `   \left[\frac{g D}{n} \frac{\partial \zeta}{\partial \xi} + {\cal F}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:477 `   \right] </math><!--\eqno{(17)}-->`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:478 `\|(17)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:479 `\|}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:480 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:481 `where the term in square brackets is the mode coupling term and is`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:482 `held fixed over all the barotropic steps and` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2310 `              rufrc(i,j)=rufrc(i,j)-rhs_ubar(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2311 `              rhs_ubar(i,j)=rhs_ubar(i,j)+rufrc(i,j)` | 일치 | 이 식은 다음 절에서 대체할 기존 방법의 설명이다. 코드의 3D 적분 우변에서 2D 우변을 빼는 절차와 대응한다. 실제 fast 압력은 rhoA·rhoS를 함께 사용한다. |

| RTN-165 | 3D momentum의 AB3·implicit viscosity·mode 교체<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:527 `The momentum equations are advanced before the tracer equation, by computing all the terms except the vertical viscosity and then using the implicit scheme described in [[#Vertical Friction and Diffusion]] to find the new values for <math>u</math> and <math>v</math>. The depth-averaged component is then removed and replaced by the <math>\langle \overline{u} \rangle</math> and <math>\langle \overline{v} \rangle</math> computed as in [[#Depth-Integrated Equations]]. A third-order Adams-Bashforth (AB3) time step is used, requiring multiple right-hand-side time levels (see [[Time-stepping Schemes Review]]). These stored up r.h.s. values can be used to extrapolate to a value at time <math>n+\frac{1}{2}</math> for use in the barotropic steps as shown in [[#Time-Stepping Overview]].` | The corrector step can maintain both, as long as it uses velocities and column depths which satisfy eq. (13). This also requires tracer values centered at time <math>n+\frac{1}{2}</math>, obtained from the predictor step. | RTN-169 | 이류 flux 발산 무번호 식<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:536 `==Horizontal and Vertical Advection==`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:537 `The advection of a tracer <math>C</math> has an equation of the form`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:538 ``<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:539 `<math display="block"> `<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:540 `  \frac{\partial}{\partial t} \frac{ H_z C}{mn} =`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:541 `  - \frac{\partial}{\partial \xi} F^\xi`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:542 `  - \frac{\partial}{\partial \eta} F^\eta`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:543 `  - \frac{\partial}{\partial \sigma} F^\sigma ,`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:544 `</math>` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:905 `                cff=dt(ng)*pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:906 `                cff1=cff*(FX(i+1,j)-FX(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:907 `                cff2=cff*(FE(i,j+1)-FE(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:908 `                cff3=cff1+cff2`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:909 `                t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)=t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)-cff3`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1348 `              CF(i,0)=dt(ng)*pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1349 `            END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1350 `            DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1351 `              DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1352 `                cff1=CF(i,0)*(FC(i,k)-FC(i,k-1))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1353 `                t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)=t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)-cff1` | 일치 | 코드는 수평 FX·FE와 연직 FC의 차를 추적자에서 뺀다. 코드의 층 적분 농도에는 Hz가 포함된다. |

| RTN-178 | U3의 논문 계열·속도 비선형 보정 생략<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:621 `There is a class of third-order upwind advection schemes, both one-dimensional ([[Bibliography#LeonardBP_1979 \|Leonard, 1979]]) and two-dimensional ([[Bibliography#RaschP_1994 \|Rasch, 1994]] and [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_1998a \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams, 1998]]). This scheme is known as UTOPIA (Uniformly Third-Order Polynomial Interpolation Algorithm). Applying flux limiters to UTOPIA is explored in [[Bibliography#ThuburnJ_1995 \| Thuburn (1995)]], although it is not implemented in ROMS. The two-dimensional formulation in Rasch contains terms of order <math>u^2C</math> and <math>u^3C</math>, including cross terms (<math>uvC</math>). The terms which are nonlinear in velocity have been dropped in ROMS, leaving one extra upwind term in the computation of the advective fluxes:` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:688 `                  FX(i,j)=Huon(i,j,k)*0.5_r8*                           &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:689 `     &                    (t(i-1,j,k,3,itrc)+                           &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:690 `     &                     t(i  ,j,k,3,itrc))-                          &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:691 `     &                    cff1*(curv(i-1,j)*MAX(Huon(i,j,k),0.0_r8)+    &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:692 `     &                          curv(i  ,j)*MIN(Huon(i,j,k),0.0_r8))` | 일치 | tracer의 U3 보정은 수송량에 선형이다. 코드의 추가 항은 upstream 곡률이다. 문서는 Thuburn limiter를 구현하지 않았다고 한정한다. |

| RTN-180 | upstream 값 선택 식 (23)<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:630 `The second derivative terms are centered on a <math>\rho</math> point in the grid, but are needed at a <math>u</math> or <math>v</math> point in the flux. The upstream value is used:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:633 `\|<math display="block">`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:634 `   F^\xi_{i,j,k} = \frac{\overline{H_z}^\xi}{\overline{n}^\xi}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:635 `   \left[ \max(0,u_{i,j,k}) C_{i-1,j,k} +`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:636 `   \min(0,u_{i,j,k}) C_{i,j,k} \right] .`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:637 `</math><!--\eqno{(23)}-->` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:688 `                  FX(i,j)=Huon(i,j,k)*0.5_r8*                           &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:689 `     &                    (t(i-1,j,k,3,itrc)+                           &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:690 `     &                     t(i  ,j,k,3,itrc))-                          &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:691 `     &                    cff1*(curv(i-1,j)*MAX(Huon(i,j,k),0.0_r8)+    &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:692 `     &                          curv(i  ,j)*MIN(Huon(i,j,k),0.0_r8))` | 불일치 | 식 (23)을 C 농도 그대로 읽으면 first-order upwind이다. 코드는 중심 농도 평균을 기본값으로 사용한다. ### 2.15 연직속도와 상태 방정식의 나머지 항목

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-189 | 수직속도·밀도 계산 시점<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:687 `Having obtained a complete specification of the <math>u,v,T,</math> and <math>S</math> fields at the next time level by the methods outlined above, the vertical velocity and density fields can be calculated. The vertical velocity`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:688 `is obtained by rewriting equation (5) as:` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:357 `                CALL set_massflux (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:358 `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:359 `                CALL rho_eos (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1026 `                CALL omega (ng, tile, iNLM)` | 불일치 | 문서는 다음 단계의 u·v·T·S를 모두 얻은 뒤 수직속도와 밀도를 계산한다고 적는다. NONLINEAR·SOLVE3D의 기본 main3d 경로는 omega를 tracer corrector 전에도 호출한다. rho_eos와 step3d_t 호출은 각각 `# ifndef TS_FIXED` 안에 있다.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:2 `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1058 `# ifndef TS_FIXED` [10-14 결론 검증 정정] [10-14 결론 검증 보완] |
| RTN-190 | 연직 연속식의 ∂tζ 무번호 식<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:690 `<math display="block">\frac{\partial}{\partial t} \left( \frac{\zeta}{m n} \right)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:691 `   + \delta_{\xi} \left(`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:692 `   \frac{u \overline{H_z}^\xi }{\overline{n}^\xi} \right)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:693 `   + \delta_{\eta} \left(`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:694 `   \frac{v \overline{H_z}^\eta}{\overline{m}^\eta} \right)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:695 `   + \delta_\sigma \left( `<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:696 `   \frac{H_z \Omega}{m n} \right) = 0 </math>` | ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:280 `          wrk(i)=W(i,j,N(ng))/(z_w(i,j,N(ng))-z_w(i,j,0))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:295 `            W(i,j,k)=W(i,j,k)-                                          &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:296 `# if defined WEC_VF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:297 `     &               W_stokes(i,j,k)-                                   &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:298 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:299 `     &               wrk(i)*(z_w(i,j,k)-z_w(i,j,0))` | 불일치 | 위키는 식 (5)의 ∂tH_z를 가중 없이 ∂tζ로 바꾼다. code는 (z_w(k)-z_w(0))/D의 이동 면 비율을 사용한다. |
| RTN-191 | 수심 적분 발산을 빼는 무번호 식<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:698 `and combining it with equation (6) to obtain:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:700 `<math display="block">`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:701 `   \frac{\partial}{\partial \xi} \left( \frac{H_z u}{n} \right) +`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:702 `   \frac{\partial}{\partial \eta} \left( \frac{H_z v}{m} \right) +`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:703 `   \frac{\partial}{\partial \sigma}\left( \frac{H_z \Omega}{mn} \right)`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:704 `   - \frac{\partial}{\partial \xi} \left( \frac{D \overline{u}}{n}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:705 `   \right) -`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:706 `   \frac{\partial}{\partial \eta} \left( \frac{D \overline{v}}{m}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:707 `   \right)  = 0 .`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:708 `</math>` | ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:225 `            W(i,j,k)=W(i,j,k-1)-                                        &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:226 `# if defined SEDIMENT && defined SED_MORPH`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:227 `     &               wrk(i)-                                            &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:228 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:229 `     &               (Huon(i+1,j,k)-Huon(i,j,k)+                        &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:230 `     &                Hvom(i,j+1,k)-Hvom(i,j,k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:280 `          wrk(i)=W(i,j,N(ng))/(z_w(i,j,N(ng))-z_w(i,j,0))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/omega.F:299 `     &               wrk(i)*(z_w(i,j,k)-z_w(i,j,0))` | 불일치 | 해석: 전체 2D 발산을 각 σ층의 3D 발산에서 그대로 빼면 σ 적분과 층 두께 단위가 맞지 않는다. 코드는 총발산을 D로 나누고 실제 깊이 비율로 배분한다. |
| RTN-192 | 연직 flux 적분 무번호 식<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:710 `Solving for <math>H_z \Omega / mn</math> and using the semi-discrete notation we obtain:`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:712 `<math display="block">`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:713 `   \frac{H_z \Omega}{mn} =  \int  \left[`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:714 `   \delta_{\xi} \left( \frac{\overline{u} \overline{D}^{\xi}}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:715 `   {\overline{n}^{\xi}} \right) +`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:716 `   \delta_{\eta} \left( \frac{\overline{v} \overline{D}^{\eta}}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:717 `   {\overline{m}^{\eta}} \right) -`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:718 `   \delta_{\xi} \left( \frac{u \overline{H_z}^{\xi}}`<br>Numerical_Solution_Technique.wiki:719 `   {\overline{n}^{\xi}} \right) - OMEGA_IMPLICIT 분할 전의 저면 누적값을 B(k), 수면부터의 누적값을 T(k), 저면부터 해당 면까지의 수심 비율을 q로 두면 T(k)=B(k)-B(N)이다. 코드의 B(k)-qB(N)은 (1-q)B(k)+qT(k)와 같다. 코드는 별도 수면 누적합을 저장하지 않지만, 깊이 비율로 가중한 양방향 적분과 대수적으로 동치이다. 기존 B 판정은 계산 절차 차이를 값의 차이로 해석했으므로 F로 정정한다.
