### 2.16 시간 적분 방식의 식 검토

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-196 | 시간 적분의 기본 식 (1)·변수<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:2 `Numerical time stepping uses a discrete approximation to: `<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:3 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:4 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:5 `\|<math display="block"> \frac{\partial \phi(t)}{\partial t} = {\cal F}(t) </math><!--\eqno{(1)}-->`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:6 `\|(1)`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:7 `\|}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:8 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:9 `where <math>\phi</math> represents one of <math>u</math>, <math>v</math>, <math>C</math>, or <math>\zeta</math> and <math>{\cal F}(t)</math> represents all the right-hand-side terms. In ROMS, the goal is to find time-stepping schemes which are accurate where they are valid and damping on unresolved signals ([[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2005a \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams (2005)]] and [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2008b \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams (2009)]]). Also, the preference is for time-stepping schemes requiring only one set of the right-hand-side terms so that different time-stepping schemes can be used for different terms in the equations. Finally, as mentioned in Table [[Numerical_Solution_Technique#Table_timestep1 \| Time Step]], not all versions of ROMS use the same time-stepping algorithm. We list some time-stepping schemes here which are used or have been used by the ROMS/SCRUM family of models, plus a few to help explain some of the more esoteric ones.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:943 `            zeta_new(i,j)=zeta(i,j,kstp)+                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:944 `     &                    pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*cff1*rhs_zeta(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:359 `            u(i,j,k,nnew)=u(i,j,k,nnew)+                                &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:360 `     &                    DC(i,0)*ru(i,j,k,nrhs)` | 일치 | F는 각 변수의 우변이다. 문서는 ROMS/SCRUM 계열의 현재·과거·설명용 방식을 함께 소개한다고 명시한다. 이 표는 모든 방식을 현행 선택 옵션으로 보지 않는다. |
| RTN-197 | Euler 식 (2)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:11 `== Euler ==`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:12 `The simplest approximation is the Euler time step:`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:13 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:14 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:15 `\|<math display="block"> \frac{\phi(t + \Delta t) - \phi(t)}{\Delta t} = {\cal F}(t) </math><!--\eqno{(2)}-->`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:16 `\|(2)`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:17 `\|}` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:937 `      IF (FIRST_2D_STEP) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:938 `        cff1=dtfast(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:939 `        DO j=JstrV-1,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:940 `          DO i=IstrU-1,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:941 `            rhs_zeta(i,j)=(DUon(i,j)-DUon(i+1,j))+                      &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:942 `     &                    (DVom(i,j)-DVom(i,j+1))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:943 `            zeta_new(i,j)=zeta(i,j,kstp)+                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:944 `     &                    pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*cff1*rhs_zeta(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:347 `        IF (iic(ng).eq.ntfirst(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:348 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)` | 일치 | code의 첫 fast predictor는 dtfast×현재 우변을 더한다. 3D AB3도 시작할 때 현재 우변만 사용한다. |
| RTN-198 | Euler의 1차 정확도·이류 불안정<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:19 `where you predict the next <math>\phi</math> value based only on the current fields.  This method is accurate to first order in <math>\Delta t</math>; however, it is unconditionally unstable with respect to advection.` | 계산 대응 없음. | 문서에만 | 이 문장은 solver의 특정 실행 결과를 주장하지 않는다. |
| RTN-199 | Leapfrog 식 (3)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:21 `== Leapfrog ==`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:22 `The leapfrog time step is accurate to O(<math>\Delta t^2</math>):`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:23 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:24 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:25 `\|<math display="block"> \frac{\phi(t + \Delta t) - \phi(t - \Delta t)}{2\Delta t} = {\cal F}(t). </math><!--\eqno{(3)}-->`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:26 `\|(3)`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:27 `\|}` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:961 `      ELSE IF (PREDICTOR_2D_STEP(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:962 `        cff1=2.0_r8*dtfast(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:963 `        cff4=4.0_r8/25.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:964 `        cff5=1.0_r8-2.0_r8*cff4`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:965 `        DO j=JstrV-1,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:966 `          DO i=IstrU-1,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:967 `            rhs_zeta(i,j)=(DUon(i,j)-DUon(i+1,j))+                      &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:968 `     &                    (DVom(i,j)-DVom(i,j+1))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:969 `            zeta_new(i,j)=zeta(i,j,kstp)+                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:970 `     &                    pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*cff1*rhs_zeta(i,j)` | 일치 | 첫 단계 뒤 predictor는 2 dtfast와 과거 상태를 사용한다. |
| RTN-200 | Leapfrog의 확산 불안정·computational mode·SCRUM<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:29 `This time step is more accurate, but it is unconditionally unstable with respect to diffusion.  Also, the even and odd time steps tend to diverge in a computational mode. This computational mode can be damped by taking correction steps.  SCRUM's time step on the depth-integrated equations was a leapfrog step with a trapezoidal correction (LF-TR) on every step, which uses a leapfrog step to obtain an initial guess of <math>\phi(t+\Delta t)</math>.  We will call the right-hand-side terms calculated from this initial guess <math>{\cal F}^*(t+\Delta t)</math>:` | 계산 대응 없음. | 문서에만 | 현행 코드의 diffusion은 별도 implicit 경로이다. SCRUM의 모든 과거 경로를 이 소스에서 찾는 작업으로 확장하지 않는다. |
| RTN-201 | LF-TR corrector 식 (4)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:32 `\|<math display="block"> \frac{\phi(t + \Delta t) - \phi(t)}{\Delta t} = \frac{1}{2}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:33 `  \left[ {\cal F}(t) + {\cal F}^*(t+\Delta t) \right]. </math><!--\eqno{(4)}-->` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:989 `        cff1=dtfast(ng)*5.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:990 `        cff2=dtfast(ng)*8.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:991 `        cff3=dtfast(ng)*1.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:992 `        cff4=2.0_r8/5.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:993 `        cff5=1.0_r8-cff4`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:994 `        DO j=JstrV-1,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:995 `          DO i=IstrU-1,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:996 `            cff=cff1*((DUon(i,j)-DUon(i+1,j))+                          &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:997 `     &                (DVom(i,j)-DVom(i,j+1)))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:998 `            zeta_new(i,j)=zeta(i,j,kstp)+                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:999 `     &                    pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*(cff+                         &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:1000 `     &                                     cff2*rzeta(i,j,kstp)-        &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:1001 `     &                                     cff3*rzeta(i,j,ptsk))` | 문서에만 | code의 기본 가중치는 5/12,8/12,-1/12이다. 문서도 이 식을 SCRUM의 방식으로 소개한다. |
| RTN-202 | LF-TR의 안정성·2회 우변 평가<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:37 `This leapfrog-trapezoidal time step is stable with respect to diffusion and it strongly damps the computational mode.  However, the right-hand-side terms are computed twice per time step.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:848 `                    CALL step2d (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:897 `                    CALL step2d (ng, tile)` | 문서에만 | 우변 2회 평가라는 절차 비교는 가능하다. 이를 현행 LF-TR 선택지로 표시하지 않는다. |
| RTN-203 | AB3 식 (5)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:39 `== Third-order Adams-Bashforth (AB3) ==`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:40 `The time step on SCRUM's full 3-D fields is done with a third-order Adams-Bashforth step.  It uses three time-levels of the right-hand-side terms:`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:41 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:42 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:43 `\|<math display="block"> \frac{\phi(t + \Delta t) - \phi(t)}{\Delta t} =`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:44 `  \alpha {\cal F}(t) +`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:45 `  \beta  {\cal F}(t - \Delta t) +`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:46 `  \gamma {\cal F}(t - 2 \Delta t) </math><!--\eqno{(5)}-->`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:47 `\|(5)`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:48 `\|}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:49 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:50 `where the coefficients <math>\alpha</math>, <math>\beta</math> and <math>\gamma</math> are chosen to obtain a third-order estimate of <math>\phi(t + \Delta t)</math>.  We use a Taylor series expansion:` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:352 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)*23.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1087 `          cff1= 5.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1088 `          cff2=16.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1089 `          DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1090 `            DO i=IstrU,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1091 `              cff3=u(i,j,k,nstp)*0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i-1,j,k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1092 `              cff4=FC(i,k)-FC(i,k-1)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1093 `              u(i,j,k,nnew)=cff3+                                       &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1094 `     &                      DC(i,0)*(cff1*ru(i,j,k,nrhs)-               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1095 `     &                               cff2*ru(i,j,k,indx))+              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1096 `     &                      cff4` | 일치 | 현재 우변과 두 과거 우변의 계수는 AB3에 대응한다. pre_step3d와 step3d_uv가 세 기여를 나누어 적용한다. |
| RTN-204 | AB3 Taylor 식 (6)의 괄호<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:53 `\|<math display="block"> \frac{\phi(t + \Delta t) - \phi(t)}{\Delta t} =`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:54 `   \phi^{\prime} + \frac{\Delta t}{2} \phi^{\prime \prime} +`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:55 `   \frac{\Delta t^2}{6} \phi^{\prime \prime \prime} + \cdots )</math><!--\eqno{(6)}-->` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:352 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)*23.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1087 `          cff1= 5.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1088 `          cff2=16.0_r8/12.0_r8` | 불일치 | 위키 식 끝은 +\cdots )이다. 계수 유도에 추가 괄호는 필요 없다. 계산 계수와 무관한 수식 오기이다. |
| RTN-205 | 세 우변의 Taylor 전개 무번호 식<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:61 `<math display="block"> \begin{align}{\cal F}(t) & = \phi^{\prime} \\`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:62 `   {\cal F}(t - \Delta t) & = \phi^{\prime}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:63 `   - \Delta t \phi^{\prime \prime}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:64 `   + \frac{\Delta t^2}{2} \phi^{\prime \prime \prime} + \cdots \\`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:65 `   {\cal F}(t - 2\Delta t) & = \phi^{\prime}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:66 `   - 2\Delta t \phi^{\prime \prime}`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:67 `   + 2\Delta t^2 \phi^{\prime \prime \prime} + \cdots \end{align}</math>` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:352 `          cff=0.25_r8*dt(ng)*23.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1087 `          cff1= 5.0_r8/12.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:1088 `          cff2=16.0_r8/12.0_r8` | 일치 | 해석: n-1과 n-2 전개를 식 (5)에 대입하면 다음 행의 세 계수를 얻는다. code 계수는 이 조건을 만족한다. |

| RTN-208 | coupled ζ·u 식 (7)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:79 `== Forward-Backward ==`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:80 `In equation (1) above, we assume that multiple equations for any number of variables are time stepped synchronously. Consider these equations:`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:81 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:82 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:83 `\|<math display="block"> \begin{align}\frac{\partial \zeta}{\partial t} &= {\cal F}(u) \\`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:84 `   \frac{\partial u}{\partial t} &= {\cal G}(\zeta) \end{align}</math><!--\eqno{(7)}-->`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:85 `\|(7)`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:86 `\|}` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:943 `            zeta_new(i,j)=zeta(i,j,kstp)+                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:944 `     &                    pm(i,j)*pn(i,j)*cff1*rhs_zeta(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:945 `#ifdef MASKING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:946 `            zeta_new(i,j)=zeta_new(i,j)*rmask(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:947 `#endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:948 `            Dnew(i,j)=zeta_new(i,j)+h(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:949 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:950 `            zwrk(i,j)=0.5_r8*(zeta(i,j,kstp)+zeta_new(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2577 `            ubar(i,j,knew)=(ubar(i,j,kstp)*                             &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2578 `     &                      (Dstp(i,j)+Dstp(i-1,j))+                    &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:2579 `     &                      cff*cff1*rhs_ubar(i,j))*fac` | 일치 | fast 경로는 ζ를 먼저 갱신해 수면 기여를 정한다. 운동량은 그 정보를 사용한다. |
| RTN-209 | pure Forward-Backward 식 (8)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:88 `If we time step them alternately, we can always be using the newest information:`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:89 ``<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:90 `{\| class="eqno"`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:91 `\|<math display="block"> \begin{align}\zeta^{n+1} &= \zeta^n + {\cal F}(u^n) \Delta t \\`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:92 `   u^{n+1} &= u^n + {\cal G}(\zeta^{n+1}) \Delta t \end{align}</math><!--\eqno{(8)}-->`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:93 `\|(8)`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:94 `\|}` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:594 `        fwd0=1.0_r8                      ! kold  kbak  kstp  knew`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:595 `        fwd1=0.0_r8                      ! fwd2  fwd1  fwd0`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:596 `        fwd2=0.0_r8                      ! bkw2  bkw1  bkw0  bkw_new`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:597 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:598 `        bkw_new=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:599 `        bkw0=1.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:600 `#else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:601 `        bkw_new=1.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:602 `        bkw0=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:603 `#endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:604 `        bkw1=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:605 `        bkw2=0.0_r8` | 문서에만 | !SOLVE3D의 분리 파일은 현재 수송으로 ζ를 갱신하고 새 수면 압력을 사용한다. step2d.F:20은 이 파일을 포함하지 않는다. |

Modified RK2 weighting:`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:771 `          cff1=0.333333333333_r8               ! here "zwrk" is time-`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:772 `          cff2=0.333333333333_r8               ! centered at "n+1/2".`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:773 `          cff3=0.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:774 `        ELSE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:775 `          cff =1.0_r8-epsil                    ! zwrk is always time-`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:776 `          cff1=(0.5_r8-gamma)*epsil            ! centered at n+1/2`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:777 `          cff2=(0.5_r8+2.0_r8*gamma)*epsil     ! during corrector sub-`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:778 `          cff3=-gamma *epsil                   ! step.` | 문서에만 | 분리 파일은 FIRST_2D_STEP의 corrector에서 세 수면 값에 각각 0.333333333333을 곱한다. 이것만으로 위키 식 (10) 전체와의 동일성을 뜻하지 않는다. 기본 include는 legacy LF-AM3이다.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:792 `            zwrk(i,j)=cff *zeta(i,j,krhs)+                              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:793 `     &                cff1*zeta_new(i,j)+                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:794 `     &                cff2*zeta(i,j,kstp)+                              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB_LF_AM3.h:795 `     &                cff3*zeta(i,j,kbak)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d.F:20 `# include "step2d_LF_AM3.h"` [10-14 결론 검증 정정] [10-14 결론 검증 보완] |
| RTN-213 | RK2-FB β·ε·αmax<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:119 `Setting <math>\beta = \epsilon = 0</math> in the above, it becomes a standard second order Runge-Kutta scheme, which is unstable for a non-dissipative system. Adding the <math>\beta</math> and <math>\epsilon</math> terms adds Forward-Backward feedback to this algorithm, and allows us to improve both its accuracy and stability. \zeta^{n+1,\star} &= \zeta^{n-1} + 2{\cal F}(u^n) \Delta t \\`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:126 `   u^{n+1,\star} &= u^{n-1} + 2\left\{ (1-2\beta){\cal | RTN-218 | Generalized FB 식 (13)<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:155 `== Generalized FB with an AB3-AM4 Step ==`<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:156 `One drawback of the previous two schemes is the need to evaluate the right-hand-side (r.h.s.) terms twice per time step. | RTN-219 | Generalized FB의 2차·3차·최대 안정 계수<br>Time-stepping_Schemes_Review.wiki:170 `This is second-order accurate for any set of values for <math>\beta</math>, <math>\gamma</math>, and <math>\epsilon</math>. [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2008b \| Shchepetkin and McWilliams (2009)]] choose to use this scheme for the barotropic mode in their model with <math>\beta= 0.281105</math>, <math>\gamma = 0.0880</math>, and <math>\epsilon = 0.013</math>, to obtain <math>\alpha_{\max} = 1.7802</math>, close to the value of 2 for pure FB, but with better stability properties for the combination of waves, advection, and Coriolis terms.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:622 `        fwd0=1.781105_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:623 `        fwd1=-1.06221_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:624 `        fwd2=0.281105_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:625 `        bkw_new=0.614_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:626 `        bkw0=0.285_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:627 `        bkw1=0.0880_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_FB.h:628 `        bkw2=0.013_r8` | 문서에만 | 문서는 Shchepetkin·McWilliams의 모델 선택이라고 적는다. αmax=1.7802는 해당 이론의 값이다. 현행 legacy solver의 허용값으로 제시하지 않는다. |

If activated, ROMS solves 3D (baroclinic) primitive equations. ### 2.18 마찰·수원·조석·수평 점성 옵션

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-233 | UV_LDRAG·rdrg·충돌·루틴<br>UV_LDRAG.wiki:1 `{{#lst:Options\|UV_LDRAG}}`<br>Options.wiki:3582 `:Option to activate/deactivate linear bottom friction of momentum.`<br>Options.wiki:3583 `:'''parameter = ''' [[Variables#rdrg \| rdrg(ng)]]`<br>Options.wiki:3584 `:'''confict = ''' [[BBL_MODEL]], [[UV_LOGDRAG]], [[UV_QDRAG]]`<br>Options.wiki:3585 `:'''routine = ''' [[set_vbc.F]]<section end=UV_LDRAG />` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:674 `#    elif defined UV_LDRAG`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:675 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:676 `!  Set linear bottom stress.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:677 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:678 `      DO j=Jstr,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:679 `        DO i=IstrU,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:680 `          bustr(i,j)=0.5_r8*(rdrag(i-1,j)+rdrag(i,j))*                  &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:681 `     &               u(i,j,1,nrhs)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:835 `#  if defined UV_LDRAG`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:836 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:837 `!  Set linear bottom stress.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:838 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:839 `      DO j=Jstr,Jend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:840 `        DO i=IstrU,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:841 `          bustr(i,j)=0.5_r8*(rdrag(i-1,j)+rdrag(i,j))*                  &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:842 `     &               ubar(i,j,krhs)`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_grid.F:1382 `            GRID(ng) % rdrag(i,j) = rdrg(ng)`<br>ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3594 `      IF (Master.and.(ibbl.gt.1)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3595 `        WRITE (stdout,40)`<br>ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3596 `  40    FORMAT (/,' CHECKDEFS - only one bottom stress formulation is', &`<br>ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3597 `     &            ' allowed.')`<br>ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3598 `        exit_flag=5`<br>ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3599 `      END IF` | 일치 | rdrg의 단위는 m s^-1이다. 코드의 rdrag는 rdrg로 초기화한다. 3D는 저면 u를 사용한다. 2D는 ubar를 사용한다. 다른 저면 마찰과 함께 선택하면 중단한다. |

| RTN-238 | UV_TIDES·ADD_M2OBC·RAMP_TIDES<br>UV_TIDES.wiki:1 `{{#lst:Options\|UV_TIDES}}`<br>Options.wiki:3618 `:Option to impose tidal currents for tidal forcing at open boundaries.`<br>Options.wiki:3622 `:'''related = ''' [[ADD_M2OBC]], [[RAMP_TIDES]]` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:249 `# ifdef RAMP_TIDES`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:250 `        ramp=TANH((tdays(ng)-dstart)/1.0_r8)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:251 `# else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:252 `        ramp=1.0_r8`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:253 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:429 `# if defined UV_TIDES`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:430 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:431 `!-----------------------------------------------------------------------`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:432 `! ### 2.19 NTIMES·TIDE_START·TIME_REF

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |

| RTN-243 | NTIMES의 수량·키워드·2D/3D 의미<br>NTIMES.wiki:2 `{{#lst:Variables\|ntimes}}`<br>Variables.wiki:1787 `:Total number time-steps in current run. If 3D configuration, ntimes is the total of baroclinic time-steps.  If only 2D configuration, ntimes  is the total of barotropic time-steps.`<br>Variables.wiki:1788 `:'''option =''' `<br>Variables.wiki:1789 `:'''routine =''' [[mod_scalars.F]]`<br>Variables.wiki:1790 `:'''keyword =''' NTIMES`<br>Variables.wiki:1791 `:'''input =''' [[roms.in]]<section end=ntimes />`<br>NTIMES.wiki:6 `'''ntimes''' (set by <span class="blue">NTIMES</span> keyword in the [[roms.in]] file) is the total number of baroclinic timesteps in the current run. In a strictly barotropic configuration (i.e CPP option [[Options#SOLVE3D\|SOLVE3D]] is undefined) '''ntimes''' is the total number of barotropic timesteps. ` | ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:585 `            CASE ('NTIMES')`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:586 `              Npts=load_i(Nval, Rval, Ngrids, ntimes)`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:336 `!  Total number timesteps in current run. In 3D configurations, "ntimes"`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:337 `!  is the total of baroclinic timesteps. In 2D configuration, "ntimes"`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:338 `!  is the total of barotropic timesteps.`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:339 `!`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:340 `        integer, allocatable :: ntimes(:)` | 일치 | NTIMES는 ntimes 배열을 읽는다. SOLVE3D에서는 baroclinic 단계 수이다. 2D 구성에서는 barotropic 단계 수이다. |
| RTN-244 | NTIMES가 절대 종료 timestep이라는 설명<br>NTIMES.wiki:8 `'''Note that ntimes determines the timestep on which the run will ''end'''''.  Be aware that time step counting does not necessarily always start from 1. This could be so if [[ocean_time]] in the initial conditions file is not zero such as when:`<br>NTIMES.wiki:9 `:# You restart a crashed run from a Restart or History file`<br>NTIMES.wiki:10 `:# You chose a reference time for [[ocean_time]] that precedes the time range you wish to simulate.` | ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:433 `        IF (msg.eq.1) THEN            ! processing initial conditions`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:434 `          time(ng)=INPtime`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:435 `          tdays(ng)=time(ng)*sec2day`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:436 `          ntstart(ng)=NINT((time(ng)-dstart*day2sec)/dt(ng))+1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:437 `          IF (ntstart(ng).lt.1) ntstart(ng)=1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:438 `          ntend(ng)=ntstart(ng)+ntimes(ng)-1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:439 `          IF (PerfectRST(ng)) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:440 `            ntfirst(ng)=1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:441 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:442 `            ntfirst(ng)=ntstart(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:172 `        ntstart(ng)=INT((time(ng)-dstart*day2sec)/dt(ng))+1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:173 `        ntend(ng)=ntstart(ng)+ntimes(ng)-1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:174 `        ntfirst(ng)=ntstart(ng)` | 불일치 | NTIMES를 절대 종료 단계로 사용한 설명은 ROMS 3.6과 일치한다. 2017-07-18 변경 뒤 종료 단계 식은 `ntend(ng)=ntstart(ng)+ntimes(ng)-1`이다.<br>git 이력 판정: e44e1f54 (2017-07-18, src:ticket:735)이 초기 상태를 읽은 뒤 상대 종료 단계를 설정했다. [판정 원문](ROMS-git-verify-A8.md).<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:126 `      ntstart(ng)=INT((time(ng)-dstart*day2sec)/dt(ng))+1`<br>roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:127 `      ntend(ng)=ntimes(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:172 `        ntstart(ng)=INT((time(ng)-dstart*day2sec)/dt(ng))+1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/initial.F:173 `        ntend(ng)=ntstart(ng)+ntimes(ng)-1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:438 `          ntend(ng)=ntstart(ng)+ntimes(ng)-1` [10-14 결론 검증 보완] [10-14 git 이력 판정] |
| RTN-245 | 90 days·180 seconds·7776480 예제<br>NTIMES.wiki:12 `For example, if your forcing file data uses [[ocean_time]]&nbsp;=&nbsp;"seconds since 2006-01-01 00:00:00", but you wish only to model the period from 2006-04-01 onward, you would set [[ocean_time]]&nbsp;=&nbsp;7776000 (90 days since 2006-01-01 multiplied by 86400 seconds per day) in the initial conditions netcdf file. For [[Variables#dt\|dt]] of 180 seconds (480 timesteps per day) '''ntimes'''&nbsp;=&nbsp;7776480 would cause the run to conclude on 2006-04-02, i.e. it would only run for 1 day of simulated time.  If you set '''ntimes'''&nbsp;=&nbsp;480 thinking this would be 1 day of simulation you will find ROMS will simply end immediately saying it is already done because the end time you requested predates the initial conditions. ` | ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:436 `          ntstart(ng)=NINT((time(ng)-dstart*day2sec)/dt(ng))+1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:437 `          IF (ntstart(ng).lt.1) ntstart(ng)=1`<br>ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:438 `          ntend(ng)=ntstart(ng)+ntimes(ng)-1` | 불일치 | 해석: 7776000/180=43200이다. 90×86400=7776000초이다. 86400/180=480단계이다. 문서의 7776480은 초와 단계 수를 더한 숫자이다. 이전 절대 종료 방식에서도 종료값은 43680이다. 현행 방식에서는 NTIMES=480을 사용한다. |
| RTN-246 | DSTART를 이용한 시간 이동 안내<br>NTIMES.wiki:15 `However, see [[dstart]] for more information on how to shift the internal time keeping in ROMS without upsetting your [[ocean_time]]&nbsp;=&nbsp;"seconds since yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS" convention.` | ROMS/Utility/get_state.F:436 `          ntstart(ng)=NINT((time(ng)-dstart*day2sec)/dt(ng))+1`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1026 `            CASE ('DSTART')`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1027 `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, 1, Dvalue)`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1028 `              dstart=Dvalue(1)` | 일치 | DSTART는 ntstart 계산의 기준에 들어간다. NetCDF 시간 단위 문자열의 원점은 TIME_REF로 정한다. |
| RTN-247 | TIDE_START 정의·days·키워드<br>TIDE_START.wiki:2 `{{#lst:Variables\|tide_start}}`<br>Variables.wiki:2310 `:Reference time origin for tidal forcing. This is the time used when processing input tidal model data. It is needed in routine [[set_tides.F]] to compute the correct phase lag with respect ROMS/TOMS initialization time.`<br>Variables.wiki:2311 `:'''option =''' `<br>Variables.wiki:2312 `:'''units =''' days`<br>Variables.wiki:2313 `:'''routine =''' [[mod_scalars.F]]`<br>Variables.wiki:2314 `:'''keyword =''' TIDE_START`<br>Variables.wiki:2315 `:'''input =''' [[roms.in]]<section end=tide_start />`<br>TIDE_START.wiki:6 `If the time origin for the harmonic tidal constituents is not the same as the time origin used internally by ROMS then the tidal phase will be in error. To accommodate this possibility, the parameter '''tide_start''' in units of '''days''' can be used to make the appropriate shift to the phase of the harmonic tidal forcing. The value set in [[roms.in]].`<br>TIDE_START.wiki:7 ``<br>TIDE_START.wiki:8 `The parameter is used in routine [[set_tides.F]] after conversion into seconds:` | ROMS/Modules/mod_scalars.F:292 `        real(dp) :: tide_start = 0.0_dp                  ! days`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1029 `            CASE ('TIDE_START')`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1030 `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, 1, Dvalue)`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1031 `              tide_start=Dvalue(1)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:280 `        cff=2.0_r8*pi*(time(ng)-tide_start*day2sec)` | 일치 | tide_start의 내부 저장 단위는 days이다. set_tides는 day2sec를 곱해 time과 같은 seconds로 바꾼다. NetCDF zero_phase_date가 없는 fallback의 입력값이다. |
| RTN-248 | 조석 위상 무번호 식<br>TIDE_START.wiki:10 `<math display="block">\eta = \sum_{i=1}^{N} A_i \cos \frac{2\pi (t-\text{tide}{\_}\text{start} - \phi_i)}{T_i}</math>` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:280 `        cff=2.0_r8*pi*(time(ng)-tide_start*day2sec)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:281 `        DO itide=1,NTC`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:282 `          IF (Tperiod(itide).gt.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:283 `            omega=cff/Tperiod(itide)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:284 `            DO j=JstrR,JendR`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:285 `              DO i=IstrR,IendR`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:286 `                Etide(i,j)=Etide(i,j)+                                  &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:287 `     &                     ramp*SSH_Tamp(i,j,itide)*                    &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:288 `     &                     COS(omega-SSH_Tphase(i,j,itide))`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/frc_tides.cdl:29 `		tide_period:units = "hours" ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/frc_tides.cdl:30 `		tide_period:field = "tide_period, scalar, series" ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/frc_tides.cdl:31 `	double tide_Ephase(tide_period, eta_rho, xi_rho) ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/frc_tides.cdl:32 `		tide_Ephase:long_name = "tidal elevation phase angle" ;`<br>Data/ROMS/CDL/frc_tides.cdl:33 `		tide_Ephase:units = "degrees, time of maximum elevation with respect to chosen time origin" ;` | 불일치 | 해석: 위키 φ_i를 시간 지연으로 정의하고 φ_time=φ_rad T/(2π)로 바꾸면 두 식이 같다. code의 SSH_Tphase는 위상각이다. 원 파일의 phase는 degrees이고 period는 hours이다. 같은 φ 기호를 단위 설명 없이 복사하지 않는다. |
| RTN-249 | TIDE_START=-365 예제·부호<br>TIDE_START.wiki:13 `For example, if the date corresponding to <math>\text{tdays}=0</math> in ROMS is 1-Jan-2006, i.e. [[ocean_time]] has the netcdf units attribute "days since 2006-01-01 00:00:00", but the harmonic forcing data was computed assuming a base date for time of 2005-01-01 00:00:00, then set the value in your [[roms.in]] with`<br>TIDE_START.wiki:14 ``<br>TIDE_START.wiki:15 `      TIDE_START = -365.0d0              ! days`<br>TIDE_START.wiki:16 ``<br>TIDE_START.wiki:17 `Note that TIDE_START will typically be negative for tide harmonic reference times that predate the model time origin.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:280 `        cff=2.0_r8*pi*(time(ng)-tide_start*day2sec)`<br>ROMS/Utility/tides_date.F:165 `          tide_start=Rclock%tide_DateNumber(1)-Rclock%DateNumber(1)` | 일치 | 2005-01-01은 2006-01-01보다 365일 이르다. code의 tide reference minus model reference는 -365일이다. φ를 생성한 원점이 더 이르면 음수이다. |
| RTN-250 | DSTART의 tide_start 설정 불영향<br>TIDE_START.wiki:19 `The value of [[dstart\|DSTART]] '''does not''' affect how the user sets '''tide_start'''.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:250 `        ramp=TANH((tdays(ng)-dstart)/1.0_r8)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_tides.F:280 `        cff=2.0_r8*pi*(time(ng)-tide_start*day2sec)` | 일치 | 위상은 time-tide_start day2sec이다. dstart는 RAMP_TIDES의 진폭 ramp에만 들어간다. 위상 설정과 진폭 ramp를 구분한다. |

| RTN-252 | TIME_REF 정의·양수 날짜·오후 12시 예제<br>TIME_REF.wiki:2 `{{#lst:Variables\|time_ref}}`<br>Variables.wiki:2318 `:Reference time (yyyymmdd.f) used to compute relative time: elapsed time interval since reference-time.`<br>Variables.wiki:2319 `:'''option = ''' `<br>Variables.wiki:2320 `:'''routine =''' [[mod_scalars.F]]`<br>Variables.wiki:2321 `:'''keyword =''' TIME_REF`<br>Variables.wiki:2322 `:'''input =''' [[roms.in]]<section end=time_ref />`<br>TIME_REF.wiki:6 `Reference time used to define the "units" attribute written to the output netcdf files. `<br>TIME_REF.wiki:7 ``<br>TIME_REF.wiki:8 `For so-called realistic applications where the model time corresponds to a certain calendar date, '''time_ref''' will be a floating point number > 0 in the format (yyyymmdd.f)`<br>TIME_REF.wiki:9 ``<br>TIME_REF.wiki:10 `For example, setting <span class="blue">TIME_REF</span>=20020115.5 in your [[roms.in]] will yield the following [http://www.cgd.ucar.edu/cms/eaton/cf-metadata/CF-1.0.html CF-Comliant] time units attribute:`<br>TIME_REF.wiki:11 ``<br>TIME_REF.wiki:12 `    units = "seconds since 2002-01-15 12:00:00" ` | ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1032 `            CASE ('TIME_REF')`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1033 `              Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, 1, Dvalue)`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1034 `              time_ref=Dvalue(1)`<br>ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1035 `              CALL ref_clock (time_ref)`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1028 `      IF (INT(r_time).gt.0) THEN                 ! The model clock is the elapsed time since reference time of the form`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1103 `!  'time-units since 1968-05-23 00:00:00 GMT'. It is a Truncated Julian`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1104 `!  day. It has a year length of 365.25 days.`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1105 `!`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1106 `!  The one here is known as the Gregorian Calendar. However, historically ROMS assumed that DayNumber=1 corresponded to`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1076 `!  01-Jan-0000 instead. So, there is one day shift. The equations`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1077 `!  can be manipulated to give either origin, but it is confusing. The`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1078 `!  above equations are cleaner and now effective (HGA: 30-Jan-2018). 'time-units since 0000-12-30 00:00:00'`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1085 `!`<br>ROMS/Utility/dateclock.F:1086 `!  to fix the one date shift because DayNumber=0 on 01-Jan-0000. ### 2.21 Old_Intro의 물리·수치 설명

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-268 | ROMS의 성격·적용 논문<br>Old_Intro.wiki:2 `ROMS is a free-surface, terrain-following, primitive equations ocean model widely used by the scientific community for a diverse range of applications (''e.g.'', [[Bibliography#HaidvogelDB_2000a \| Haidvogel et al., 2000]]; [[Bibliography#MarchesielloP_2003a \| Marchesiello et al., 2003]]; [[Bibliography#PelizA_2003a \| Peliz et al., 2003]]; [[Bibliography#DiLorenzoE_2003a \| Di Lorenzo, 2003]]; [[Bibliography#DinnimanMS_2003a \| Dinniman et al., 2003]]; [[Bibliography#BudgellWP_2005a \| Budgell, 2005]]; [[Bibliography#WarnerJC_2005a \| Warner et al., 2005a]], [[Bibliography#WarnerJC_2005b \| b]]; [[Bibliography#WilkinJL_2005a \| Wilkin et al., 2005]]).` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:12 `!  This routine is the main driver for ROMS nonlinear model (NLM)      !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:13 `!  when configurated as a full 3D baroclinic ocean model. It advances  !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:14 `!  forward the NLM for all nested grids, if any, for the specified     !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:15 `!  time interval (seconds), RunInterval.                               !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:12 `!  This routine computes the time evolving depths of the model grid    !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:13 `!  and its associated vertical transformation metric (thickness).      !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:14 `!                                                                      !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:15 `!  Currently, two vertical coordinate transformations are available    !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:16 `!  with various possible vertical stretching, C(s), functions, (see    !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:17 `!  routine "set_scoord.F" for details).                                !` | 문서에만 | 모델의 성격은 code와 대응한다. 널리 쓰인 적용의 목록은 역사·적용 설명이다. |
| RTN-269 | 계산 kernel의 서지·정확도·coupled application<br>Old_Intro.wiki:3 `The algorithms that comprise ROMS computational nonlinear kernel are described in detail in Shchepetkin and McWilliams ([[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2003a \| 2003]], [[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2005a \| 2005]]), and the tangent linear and adjoint kernels and platforms are described in [[Bibliography#MooreAM_2004a \| Moore et al. (2004)]].`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:4 `ROMS includes accurate and efficient physical and numerical algorithms and several coupled models for biogeochemical ([[Bibliography#PowellTP_2006a \|Power et al., 2006]]; [[Bibliography#FennelK_2006a \|Fennel et al., 2006]]), bio-optical ([[Bibliography#BissettWP_1999a \|Bissett et al., 1999]]), sediment ([[Bibliography#WarnerJC_2006a \|Warner et al., 2006]]), and sea ice ([[Bibliography#BudgellWP_2005a \|Budgell, 2005]]) applications.` | ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:817 `#if defined BIO_FENNEL  \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:818 `    defined ECB         \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:819 `    defined ECOSIM      \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:820 `    defined HYPOXIA_SRM \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:821 `    defined NEMURO      \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:822 `    defined NPZD_FRANKS \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:823 `    defined NPZD_IRON   \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:824 `    defined NPZD_POWELL \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:825 `    defined RED_TIDE`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:826 `# define BIOLOGY`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:827 `#endif` | 문서에만 | 이 문단에는 대조할 논문 식이나 계수가 없다. 전용 결합 모델 내부의 역어셈블리로 확대하지 않는다. |
| RTN-270 | 연직 혼합·nesting·composed grid<br>Old_Intro.wiki:5 `It also includes several vertical mixing schemes ([[Bibliography#WarnerJC_2005a \|Warner et al., 2005a]]), multiple levels of nesting and composed grids.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:533 `# if defined ANA_VMIX`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:534 `                CALL ana_vmix (ng, tile, iNLM)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:535 `# elif defined LMD_MIXING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:536 `                CALL lmd_vmix (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:537 `# elif defined BVF_MIXING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:538 `                CALL bvf_mix (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1004 `# ifdef NESTING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1005 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1006 `!  If composite or mosaic grids, process additional points in the`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1007 `!  contact zone between connected grids for 3D momentum (u,v),`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1008 `!  adjusted 2D momentum (ubar,vbar), and fluxes (Huon, Hvom).`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1009 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1010 `            DO ig=1,GridsInLayer(nl)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1011 `              ng=GridNumber(ig,nl)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1012 `              IF (ANY(CompositeGrid(:,ng))) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1013 `                CALL nesting (ng, iNLM, n3duv)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1014 `              END IF` | 일치 | 코드는 여러 연직 혼합 분기를 가진다. NESTING은 composite·refined grid를 별도로 처리한다. |
| RTN-271 | split-explicit·fast 평균·aliasing 회피<br>Old_Intro.wiki:7 `For computational economy, the hydrostatic primitive equations for momentum are solved using a split-explicit time-stepping scheme which requires special treatment and coupling between barotropic (fast) and baroclinic (slow) modes.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:7 `A finite number of barotropic time steps, within each baroclinic step, are carried out to evolve the free-surface and vertically integrated momentum equations.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:7 `In order to avoid the errors associated with the aliasing of frequencies resolved by the barotropic steps but unresolved by the baroclinic step, the barotropic fields are time averaged before they replace those values obtained with a longer baroclinic step.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:819 `            LOOP_2D : DO my_iif=1,MAXVAL(nfast)+1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:812 `              Zt_avg1(i,j)=Zt_avg1(i,j)+cff1*zeta(i,j,krhs)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:813 `            END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:814 `            DO i=Istr,IendR`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:815 `              DU_avg1(i,j)=DU_avg1(i,j)+cff1*DUon(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:816 `# ifdef WEC`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:817 `              DU_avg1(i,j)=DU_avg1(i,j)-cff1*DUSon(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:818 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:819 `              DU_avg2(i,j)=DU_avg2(i,j)+cff2*DUon(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:694 `          cff1=1.0_r8/(CF(i,0)*on_u(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:695 `          DC(i,0)=(DC(i,0)*on_u(i,j)-DU_avg1(i,j))*cff1      ! recursive`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:696 `# ifdef WEC`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:697 `          cff2=1.0_r8/CF(i,0)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:698 `          DCs(i,0)=DCs(i,0)*cff2-ubar_stokes(i,j)            ! recursive`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:699 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:700 `# ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_UV`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:701 `          DO idiag=1,M2pgrd`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:702 `            Dwrk(i,idiag)=(Dwrk(i,idiag)*on_u(i,j)-                     &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:703 `     &                     DiaU2wrk(i,j,idiag))*cff1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:704 `          END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:705 `          Dwrk(i,M2bstr)=(Dwrk(i,M2bstr)*on_u(i,j)-                     &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:706 `     &                    DiaU2wrk(i,j,M2bstr)-DiaU2wrk(i,j,M2sstr))*   &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:707 `     &                   cff1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:708 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:709 `        END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:710 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:711 `!  Couple and update new solution.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:712 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:713 `        DO k=1,N(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:714 `          DO i=IstrU,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:715 `            u(i,j,k,nnew)=u(i,j,k,nnew)-DC(i,0)` | 일치 | 코드는 fast 단계를 수행하고 시간 평균 수송을 3D 평균 보정에 사용한다. |
| RTN-272 | cosine-shape filter가 사용된다는 설명<br>Old_Intro.wiki:7 `A cosine-shape time filter, centered at the new time level, is used for the averaging of the barotropic fields ([[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2005a \|Shchepetkin and McWilliams, 2005]]).` | ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:119 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:120 `# undef COSINE2`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:121 `# define POWER_LAW`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:122 `#endif` | 불일치 | Old_Intro라는 쪽 이름만으로 과거 기본 필터를 cosine이라고 판정할 수 없다. roms-2.0부터 roms-4.2까지 보존된 17개 태그와 현재 SOLVE3D의 기본 필터는 POWER_LAW이다.<br>git 이력 판정: roms-2.0 (2530ef9a)부터 roms-4.2 (f3cd98c7)까지 보존된 17개 태그에서 SOLVE3D는 COSINE2를 해제하고 POWER_LAW를 정의한다. 이 범위의 기본 필터를 cosine에서 POWER_LAW로 바꾼 판 차이로 볼 수 없다. [판정 원문](ROMS-git-verify-A8.md).<br>roms-2.0:Include/globaldefs.h:55 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>roms-2.0:Include/globaldefs.h:56 `# undef COSINE2`<br>roms-2.0:Include/globaldefs.h:57 `# define POWER_LAW`<br>roms-4.2:ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:119 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>roms-4.2:ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:120 `# undef COSINE2`<br>roms-4.2:ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:121 `# define POWER_LAW`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:119 `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:120 `# undef COSINE2`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:121 `# define POWER_LAW` [10-14 결론 검증 정정] [10-14 결론 검증 보완] [10-14 git 이력 판정] |

| RTN-276 | 연직 좌표·기본 centered2·splines·압력 경도 오차<br>Old_Intro.wiki:9 `In the vertical, the primitive equations are discretized over variable topography using stretched terrain-following coordinates ([[Bibliography#SongY_1994a \|Song and Haidvogel, 1994]]). The stretched coordinates allow increased resolution in areas of interest, such as thermocline and bottom boundary layers. The default stencil uses centered, second-order finite differences on a staggered vertical grid. Options for higher order stencil are available via a conservative, parabolic spline reconstruction of vertical derivatives ([[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2005a \|Shchepetkin and McWilliams, 2005]]). This class of model exhibits stronger sensitivity to topography which results in pressure gradient errors. These errors arise due to splitting of the pressure gradient term into an along-sigma component and a hydrostatic correction (for details, see [[Bibliography#HaidvogelDB_1999a \| Haidvogel and Beckmann, 1999]]). The numerical algorithm in ROMS is designed to reduce such errors ([[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_2003a \|Shchepetkin and McWilliams, 2003]]).` | ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:158 `            cff_r=hc(ng)*(SCALARS(ng)%sc_r(k)-SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:159 `            cff_w=hc(ng)*(SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)-SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:160 `            cff1_r=SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:161 `            cff1_w=SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:162 `            DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:163 `              hwater=h(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:164 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:165 `              hwater=hwater-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:166 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:167 `              hinv=1.0_r8/hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:168 `              z_w0=cff_w+cff1_w*hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:169 `              z_w(i,j,k)=z_w0+Zt_avg1(i,j)*(1.0_r8+z_w0*hinv)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:170 `              z_r0=cff_r+cff1_r*hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:171 `              z_r(i,j,k)=z_r0+Zt_avg1(i,j)*(1.0_r8+z_r0*hinv)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:172 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:173 `              z_w(i,j,k)=z_w(i,j,k)-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:174 `              z_r(i,j,k)=z_r(i,j,k)-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:175 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:176 `              Hz(i,j,k)=z_w(i,j,k)-z_w(i,j,k-1)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1733 `                cff1=1.0_r8/(z_r(i,j,k+1)-z_r(i,j,k))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1734 `                FC(i,k)=cff*cff1*Akt(i,j,k,ltrc)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1098 `#  ifdef UV_SADVECTION`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1099 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1100 `!  Construct conservative parabolic splines for the vertical`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/rhs3d.F:1101 `!  derivatives "CF" of u-momentum.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:16 `# if defined PJ_GRADPQ4`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:17 `#  include "prsgrd44.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:18 `# elif defined PJ_GRADPQ2`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:19 `#  include "prsgrd42.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:20 `# elif defined PJ_GRADP`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:21 `#  include "prsgrd40.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:22 `# elif defined DJ_GRADPS`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:23 `#  include "prsgrd32.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:24 `# else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/prsgrd.F:25 `#  include "prsgrd31.h"` | 일치 | 좌표와 2차 연직 구배는 code에 있다. splines는 별도 옵션이다. 압력 경도 경로도 별도로 선택한다. 오류 감소의 정량 크기는 이 문서가 적지 않는다. |
| RTN-277 | 수평 orthogonal C-grid·metric·mask·기본 차분<br>Old_Intro.wiki:11 `In the horizontal, the primitive equations are evaluated using boundary-fitted, orthogonal curvilinear coordinates on a staggered Arakawa C-grid. The general formulation of curvilinear coordinates includes both Cartesian (constant metrics) and spherical (variable metrics) coordinates. Coastal boundaries can also be specified as a finite-discretized grid via land/sea masking. As in the vertical, the horizontal stencil utilizes a centered, second-order finite differences. However, the code is designed to make the implementation of higher order stencils easily.` | ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:349 `          om_r(i,j)=1.0_r8/pm(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:350 `          on_r(i,j)=1.0_r8/pn(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:351 `          omn(i,j)=1.0_r8/(pm(i,j)*pn(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:143 `            Huon(i,j,k)=0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i-1,j,k))*u(i,j,k,nrhs)*   &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:144 `     &                  on_u(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:145 `# ifdef WEC`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:146 `            Huon(i,j,k)=Huon(i,j,k)+                                    &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:147 `     &                  0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i-1,j,k))*                 &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:148 `     &                  u_stokes(i,j,k)*on_u(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:149 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:150 `          END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:151 `        END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:152 `        DO j=JstrP,JendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:153 `          DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:154 `            Hvom(i,j,k)=0.5_r8*(Hz(i,j,k)+Hz(i,j-1,k))*v(i,j,k,nrhs)*   &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_massflux.F:155 `     &                  om_v(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1887 `              t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)=t(i,j,k,nnew,itrc)*rmask(i,j)` | 일치 | 기본 geometry 차분과 높은 차수의 이류 재구성을 구분한다. 코드의 C-grid 평균과 mask가 대응한다. |
| RTN-278 | 이류 선택·default U3·splines dispersion<br>Old_Intro.wiki:13 `ROMS has various options for advection schemes: second- and forth-order centered differences; and third-order, upstream biased. The later scheme is the model default and it has a velocity-dependent hyper-diffusion dissipation as the dominant truncation error ([[Bibliography#ShchepetkinAF_1998a \|Shchepetkin and McWilliams, 1998]]). These schemes are stable for the predictor-corrector methodology of the model. In addition, there is an option for conservative parabolic spline representation of vertical advection which has dispersion properties similar to an eight-order accurate conventional scheme.` | ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:15 `**   The default horizontal advection is 3rd-order upstream bias for         **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:16 `**   3D momentum and 4th-order centered for 2D momentum. | RTN-279 | momentum·tracer의 isopycnic mixing 공통 지원 주장<br>Old_Intro.wiki:15 `There are several subgrid-scale parameterizations in ROMS. The horizontal mixing of momentum and tracers can be along vertical levels, geopotential (constant depth) surfaces, or isopycnic (constant density) surfaces. The mixing operator can be harmonic (3-point stencil) or biharmonic (5-point stencil). See [[Bibliography#HaidvogelDB_1999a \| Haidvogel and Beckmann (1999)]] for an overview of all these operators.` | ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:219 `** MIX_S_UV                if mixing along constant                                                                        **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:224 `** DIFF_GRID               to scale diffusion coefficients by grid size "uv3dmix4_geo.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/uv3dmix.F:33 `#  else`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/uv3dmix.F:34 `      UV3DMIX: must define one of MIX_S_UV, MIX_GEO_UV` | 불일치 | 위키는 momentum과 tracer를 함께 세 방향으로 적는다. code의 momentum include에는 isopycnic 방향이 없다. harmonic과 biharmonic 선택은 별도이다. |
| RTN-280 | MY2.5·GLS·KPP·stability function·BBL<br>Old_Intro.wiki:17 `The vertical mixing parameterization in ROMS can be either by local or nonlocal closure schemes. The local closure schemes are based on the level 2.5 turbulent kinetic energy equations by [[Bibliography#MellorGL_1982a \| Mellor and Yamada (1982)]] and the [[Generic Length Scale]] (GLS) parameterization ([[Bibliography#UmlaufL_2003a \|Umlauf and Burchard, 2003]]). The nonlocal closure scheme is based on the K-profile, boundary layer formulation by [[Bibliography#LargeWG_1994a \| Large et al. (1994)]]. The K-profile scheme has been expanded to include both surface and bottom oceanic boundary layers. The GLS is a two-equation turbulence model that allows a wide range of vertical mixing closures, including the popular k-kl (Mellor-Yamada level 2.5), k-e, and k-w schemes. Several stability functions ([[Bibliography#GalperinB_1988a \|Galperin et al., 1988]]; [[Bibliography#KanthaLH_1994a \| Kantha and Clayson, 1994]]; [[Bibliography#CanutoVM_2001a \| Canuto et al., 2001]]) have been also added to provide further flexibility. A recent study ([[Bibliography#WarnerJC_2005a \|Warner et al., 2005a]]) evaluated the performance of these turbulence closures in ROMS in terms of idealized sediment transport applications. In addition, there is a wave/current bed boundary layer scheme that provides the bottom stress ([[Bibliography#StylesR_2000a \|Styles and Glenn, 2000]]) and sediment transport which become important in coastal applications.` | ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:237 `** GLS_MIXING              if Generic Length-Scale mixing closure            **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:238 `** MY25_MIXING             if Mellor/Yamada Level-2.5 closure                **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:239 `** LMD_MIXING              if Large et al. (1994) interior closure           **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:249 `** CANUTO_A                if Canuto A-stability function formulation        **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:250 `** CANUTO_B                if Canuto B-stability function formulation        **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:251 `** CHARNOK                 if Charnock surface roughness from wind stress    **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:252 `** CRAIG_BANNER            if Craig and Banner wave breaking surface flux    **`<br>ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:253 `** KANTHA_CLAYSON          if Kantha and Clayson stability function          **`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:771 `#if defined MB_BBL  \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:772 `    defined SG_BBL  \|\| \`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:773 `    defined SSW_BBL`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:774 `# define BBL_MODEL`<br>ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:775 `#endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1027 `# ifdef MY25_MIXING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1028 `                CALL my25_corstep (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1029 `# elif defined GLS_MIXING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:1030 `                CALL gls_corstep (ng, tile)` | 일치 | code는 명시한 혼합 계열과 BBL 선택을 가진다. 인용 논문의 sediment 적용 성능은 이 행의 확인 범위에 포함하지 않는다. |
| RTN-281 | Fairall·COARE bulk의 현재 판<br>Old_Intro.wiki:19 `Currently, the air-sea interaction boundary layer in ROMS is based on the bulk parameterization of [[Bibliography#FairallCW_1996a \| Fairall et al. ### 2.22 Old_Intro의 구현 구조와 framework

| 행 ID | 문서 항목과 원문 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-282 | CPP·serial·tiles·OpenMP·MPI 구조<br>Old_Intro.wiki:21 `ROMS is a very modern code and uses C-preprocessing to activate the various physical and numerical options. The code can be run in either serial or parallel computers. The code uses a coarse-grained parallelization paradigm which partitions the computational 3D grid into tiles. Each tile is then operated on by different parallel threads. Originally, the code was designed for shared-memory computer architectures and the parallel compiler-dependent directives (OpenMP Standard) are placed only in the main computational routine of the code. An MPI version of the code has been developed so both shared and distributed-memory paradigms coexist together in a single code.` | makefile:113 `     USE_MPI ?=`<br>makefile:114 `  USE_OpenMP ?=`<br>makefile:218 `%.f90: %.F`<br>makefile:219 `	$(CPP) $(CPPFLAGS) $(MY_CPP_FLAGS) $< > $*.f90`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:638 `            DO ig=1,GridsInLayer(nl)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:639 `              ng=GridNumber(ig,nl)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:640 `              DO tile=last_tile(ng),first_tile(ng),-1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:641 `                CALL rhs3d (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:642 `# ifdef MY25_MIXING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:643 `                CALL my25_prestep (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:644 `# elif defined GLS_MIXING`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:645 `                CALL gls_prestep (ng, tile)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:646 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:647 `              END DO` | 일치 | 코드는 CPP로 구성한다. 타일 루프가 계산 범위를 나눈다. OpenMP와 MPI는 같은 소스의 별도 빌드 구성이다. Originally로 한정한 개발 이력은 현재 계산의 필수 조건이 아니다. |
| RTN-283 | F90/F95·모듈·상태 동적 할당·pointer<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `ROMS is a very modern and modular code written in F90/F95.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `It uses C-preprocessing to activate the various physical and numerical options.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `All the state model variables are dynamically allocated and passed as arguments to the computational routines via de-referenced pointer structures.` | ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:459 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % t(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,N(ng),3,NT(ng)) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:460 `      Dmem(ng)=Dmem(ng)+3.0_r8*REAL(N(ng)*NT(ng),r8)*size2d`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:461 `!`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:462 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % u(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,N(ng),2) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:463 `      Dmem(ng)=Dmem(ng)+2.0_r8*REAL(N(ng),r8)*size2d`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:464 `!`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:465 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % ua(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,N(ng)) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:466 `      Dmem(ng)=Dmem(ng)+REAL(N(ng),r8)*size2d`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:467 `!`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:468 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % v(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,N(ng),2) )`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:60 `     &                     GRID(ng) % h,                                &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:61 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:62 `     &                     GRID(ng) % zice,                             &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:63 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:64 `     &                     COUPLING(ng) % Zt_avg1,                      &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:65 `     &                     GRID(ng) % Hz,                               &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:66 `     &                     GRID(ng) % z_r,                              &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:67 `     &                     GRID(ng) % z_w)` | 일치 | 모듈의 상태 배열을 allocate한다. wrapper는 GRID·OCEAN 구조체의 배열을 tile routine 인수로 넘긴다. modern과 readability는 정성 설명이다. |
| RTN-284 | 모든 private·scratch array가 automatic이라는 설명<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `All private or scratch arrays are automatic; their size is determined when the procedure is entered.` | ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d_LF_AM3.h:630 `      real(r8), dimension(IminS:ImaxS,JminS:JmaxS) :: zeta_new`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:321 `      real(r8), allocatable :: Dhadv(:,:,:)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:322 `      real(r8), allocatable :: Dvadv(:,:,:,:)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:323 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:324 `      real(r8), allocatable :: Ta(:,:,:,:)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:325 `      real(r8), allocatable :: Ua(:,:,:)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:326 `      real(r8), allocatable :: Va(:,:,:)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:327 `      real(r8), allocatable :: Wa(:,:,:)` | 불일치 | legacy zeta_new는 bounds로 크기를 정한 automatic array이다. step3d_t의 Ta·Ua·Va·Wa와 diagnostic scratch는 allocatable로 선언한다. | RTN-285 | 중첩 구성·parallel source 공존·다른 message protocol<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `This code structure facilitates computations over nested and composed grids.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `The parallel framework is coarse-grained with both shared- and distributed-memory paradigms coexisting in the same code.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `The shared-memory option follows OpenMP 2.0 standard.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `ROMS has a generic distributed-memory interface that facilitates the use of several message passage protocols.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `Currently, the data exchange between nodes is done with MPI.`<br>Old_Intro.wiki:23 `However, other protocols like MPI2, SHMEM, and others can be coded without much effort.` | makefile:381 `ifdef USE_MPI`<br>makefile:382 ` ifdef USE_OpenMP`<br>makefile:383 `  $(error You cannot activate USE_MPI and USE_OpenMP at the same time!)`<br>ROMS/Bin/build_roms.sh:324 `if [ -n "${USE_MPI:+1}" ] && [ -n "${USE_OpenMP:+1}" ]; then`<br>ROMS/Bin/build_roms.sh:325 `  echo ""`<br>ROMS/Bin/build_roms.sh:326 `  echo "You cannot activate USE_MPI and USE_OpenMP at the same time!"`<br>ROMS/Bin/build_roms.sh:327 `  exit 1` | 문서에만 | makefile와 build script는 USE_MPI·USE_OpenMP 동시 활성화를 거부한다. | RTN-295 | 1995 SCRUM·SPEM·BASIN·ROMS 개발 연혁<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:2 `In the fall of 1995,  Hernan G. Arango (hereafter <span class="blue">HGA</span>) started working at the Ocean Modeling Lab ('''OML'''), Institute of Marine and Coastal Sciences ('''IMCS'''), Rutgers University. His first assignment from '''OML''''s group leader Dale B. Haigvogel (hereafter <span class="blue">DBH</span>) was to work on the classic baroclinic, wind-driven, double gyre problem with the now-extinct '''S'''-'''C'''oordinate '''R'''utgers '''U'''niversity '''M'''odel ('''SCRUM''', Song and Haidvogel, 1994), which was derived from '''SPEM''' ('''S'''emi-spectral '''P'''rimitive '''E'''quation '''M'''odel; Haidvogel ''et al.'', 1991). The double-gyre application is affectionately known as '''Big Bad Basin''' (nowadays, <span class="mediumOrchid">BASIN</span> test case) by several '''OML''' modelers because of its stability issues in long simulations. After a couple of months of struggling with the <span class="mediumOrchid">BASIN</span> problem, <span class="blue">HGA</span> decided to rewrite '''SCRUM''' from scratch and modify its numerical kernel, including time-stepping, advection schemes, and implementing a split-explicit treatment coupling between barotropic (fast) and barotropic (slow) governing equations in collaboration with '''OML''''s scientists Robert J. Chant and Katherine S. Hedström. In addition, a complete overhaul of the model metadata, input, and output files with NetCDF format. And voilà, the <span class="mediumOrchid">BASIN</span> application ran successfully for several years. The new model became '''SCRUM''' version '''3.0''', which later evolved to '''SCRUM 4.0''' and then becomes the '''R'''egional '''O'''cean '''M'''odeling '''S'''ystem ('''ROMS'''). The acronym '''ROMS''' was inspired by the '''R'''egional '''A'''tmospheric '''M'''odeling '''S'''ystem ('''RAMS'''). Thus, at the time, there was a regional numerical model for the atmosphere and ocean with similar acronyms.` | 계산 대응 없음. | 문서에만 | 인명·소속·개발 협력·성능 일화는 현재 계산식 대조 대상이 아니다. fast·slow의 문서 오기는 다음 행에서 따로 판정한다. |
| RTN-296 | fast·slow를 모두 barotropic으로 적은 문장<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:2 `barotropic (fast) and barotropic (slow) governing equations` | ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:13 `!  when configurated as a full 3D baroclinic ocean model. It advances  !`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:813 `!  Solve the vertically integrated primitive equations for the`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:814 `!  free-surface and barotropic momentum components using a predictor-`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:815 `!  corrector LeapFrog with 3rd-order Adams-Moulton (LF-AM3) time`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:816 `!  stepping scheme.` | 불일치 | code는 full 3D baroclinic main 안에서 depth-integrated barotropic fast 경로를 호출한다. slow도 barotropic이라는 표기는 오기이다. |
| RTN-297 | 1998 UCLA 협력·OpenMP·최적화·IEEE 일화<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:4 `In 1998, James C. McWilliams (hereafter, <span class="blue">JCM</span>) started looking for a regional ocean model for the U.S. West Coast and contacted <span class="blue">DBH</span> for possible collaborations. As a result, <span class="blue">DBH</span> and <span class="blue">HGA</span> traveled to Los Angeles, to meet at University of California, Los Angeles (UCLA) with <span class="blue">JCM</span> and Alexander F. Shchepetkin  (hereafter <span class="blue">AFS</span>) or just Sasha, as the '''ROMS''' VIPs know him. The expectation that <span class="blue">HGA</span> and <span class="blue">AFS</span> would get along was very low. However, their mutual respect, skills, and friendship grew. As a result, and to the benefit of the ocean modeling community,  '''ROMS''' was officially born. Their first task was to improve its efficiency by implementing a multi-threaded parallel version of its numerical kernel using the shared-memory paradigm native to the IRIX operating system available on SGI supercomputers of the time. The shared-memory compiling directives evolved later to the portable '''OpenMP''' standard implementation in '''ROMS'''. Moreover, <span class="blue">AFS</span> began looking at the ensemble code and started counting operations; multiplications replaced unnecessary divisions when appropriate, and '''DO'''-loops were reorganized and pipelined to reduce memory hits. <span class="blue">HGA</span> learned how to count operations and analyzed optimized Fortran code, which was available at the time, to minimize the need for automatic optimization. As a result, the '''ROMS''' code was very efficient and fast. Nowadays, '''ROMS''' can be compiled with aggressive Fortran optimization flags. Of course, users need to be aware that the bold optimization flags in some compilers could compromise the numerical '''IEEE''' floating-point precision, and new flags are required to retain it, which may slow down the computations.` | 계산 대응 없음. | 문서에만 | 이 행은 협력·연산 수·compiler 최적화의 개발 연혁과 성능 일화이다. [10-14 결론 검증 정정] |
| RTN-298 | 2000 ONR·ROMS-DA 협력·그룹 그림<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:6 `In 2000, Terri Paluszkiewicz from the Office of Naval Research (ONR) approached <span class="blue">HGA</span> and <span class="blue">JCM</span> about funding opportunities to develop the next generation of a regional, high-resolution ocean model with accurate numerical algorithms and data assimilation capabilities. As a result, ONR started funding '''ROMS''' development separately to Rutgers (data assimilation and adjoint-based algorithms, applications, maintenance, and community outreach) and UCLA (numerical algorithms and applications). Then, <span class="blue">HGA</span> suggested to ONR that a redesign of '''ROMS''' was required, and a team of experts was needed to do variational data assimilation correctly. During his years at Harvard, <span class="blue">HGA</span> had the opportunity to meet Andrew M. Moore (hereafter, <span class="blue">AMM</span>), now at the University of California, Santa Cruz (UCSC), and work with Arthur J. Miller (hereafter, <span class="blue">AJM</span>) from the Scripps Institute of Oceanography (SIO). <span class="blue">AMM</span> is an expert on adjoint-based modeling and applications. Therefore, the <span class="blue">ROMS</span> data assimilation team included <span class="blue">AMM</span>, <span class="blue">HGA</span>, <span class="blue">AJM</span>, collaborator Bruce D. Cornuelle (hereafter, <span class="blue">BDC</span>), and Ph. D. student Emanuele Di Lorenzo (hereafter, <span class="blue">EDL</span>), and Douglas J. Neilson (Hereafter, <span class="blue">DJW</span>). ONR also funded UCSC and SIO separately for this work. Thus, it started a very successful collaboration with several peer-reviewed publications in journals and books. The chemistry between the '''ROMS''' Data Assimilation ('''ROMS-DA''') group was extraordinary, with lots of fun, good food, and great wine-tasting parties.`<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:8 `[[Image:AdjointGroup.jpg\|1000px\|center]]`<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:9 `<center>'''''ROMS Adjoint Group:''''' Back, left to right: Arthur J. Miller, Andrew M. Moore, Bruce D. Cornuelle, and Douglas J. Neilson. Front: Hernan G. Arango and Emanuel Di Lorenzo. </center>` | 계산 대응 없음. | 문서에만 | 로컬 AdjointGroup.jpg는 없다. 해당 인명이 수치 계수나 CPP 조건을 추가하지 않는다. |
| RTN-299 | hand-coded TLM·ADM·exact adjoint·L2·RPM<br>Regional_Ocean_Modeling_System__ROMS_.wiki:11 `<span class="blue">AMM</span> and <span class="blue">HGA</span> frequently traveled to Scripps, in La Jolla, for an intense week-long perturbation, tangent linear model ('''TLM''') and adjoint model ('''ADM''') code writing. <span class="blue">AMM</span> was the master teaching and directing the monkies on how to write '''ROMS-TL''' and '''ROMS-AD''' codes. The '''TLM''' and '''ADM''' were hand-coded from the discrete nonlinear ('''NLM''') code using the recipes of Giering and Kaminski (1998). Therefore, any change to its dynamical and numerical kernels will affect the symmetry of the '''TLM''' and '''ADM''' operators. Moreover, the resulting discrete adjoint is exact and is defined relative to the inner product that prescribes the '''L2'''-norm. The finite-amplitude, tangent linear code for the Representer model ('''RPM''') was later derived from the '''TLM'''. The '''RPM''' is used in the indirect, representer-based approach for 4-Dimensional Variational ('''4D-Var''') data assimilation.` | ROMS/Tangent/tl_main3d.F:12 `! ROMS when  configure as a  full 3D baroclinic ocean model.          !`<br>ROMS/Representer/rp_main3d.F:14 `! | RTN-340 | hc>hmin이 항상 음의 연직 좌표 미분을 준다는 단정<br>Vertical_S-coordinate.wiki:29 `Notice that it is not possible to build the vertical stretching coordinates when&nbsp; <math>h_{c}> h_{min}</math>&nbsp; and using transformation [[#transform1\|(1)]] because it yields &nbsp;<math>\partial{z}/\partial{\sigma} < 0</math>&nbsp;.` | ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:229 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:230 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_w(k)=SCALARS(ng)%sc_w(k)`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:231 `            SCALARS(ng)%Cs_r(k)=SCALARS(ng)%sc_r(k)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:162 `            DO i=IstrT,IendT`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:163 `              hwater=h(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:164 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:165 `              hwater=hwater-ABS(zice(i,j))`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:166 `# endif`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:167 `              hinv=1.0_r8/hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:168 `              z_w0=cff_w+cff1_w*hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:169 `              z_w(i,j,k)=z_w0+Zt_avg1(i,j)*(1.0_r8+z_w0*hinv)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:170 `              z_r0=cff_r+cff1_r*hwater`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:171 `              z_r(i,j,k)=z_r0+Zt_avg1(i,j)*(1.0_r8+z_r0*hinv)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:172 `# ifdef ICESHELF`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:173 `              z_w(i,j,k)=z_w(i,j,k)-ABS(zice(i,j))` | 불일치 | 해석: 빙붕이 없는 Vstretching=1에서 theta_s=0의 C(s)=s를 변환 (1)에 대입하면 z=ζ+(ζ+h)s이다. 따라서 h+ζ>0이면 ∂s z=h+ζ>0이다. 이 결과는 hc>h인 수학적 대입에서도 같다. 실제 코드의 비WET_DRY 분기는 hc=MIN(hmin,Tcline)을 사용한다. WET_DRY 분기는 hc=MIN(MAX(hmin,0),Tcline)을 사용한다. 문서의 구성 제한은 유지한다. 음의 미분이 반드시 발생한다는 문장은 이 반례와 다르다.<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:184 `      IF (Vstretching(ng).eq.1) THEN`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:171 `# if defined WET_DRY`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:172 `        hc(ng)=MIN(MAX(hmin(ng),0.0_dp),Tcline(ng))`<br>ROMS/Utility/set_scoord.F:174 `        hc(ng)=MIN(hmin(ng),Tcline(ng))` [10-14 결론 검증 정정] [10-14 결론 검증 보완] |


## 3 코드에만 있는 처리

이 절의 문서 누락은 §2의 대상 쪽을 기준으로 한다.
다른 ROMS 위키 전체의 누락을 뜻하지 않는다.
§2에서 이미 판정한 누락도 관련 처리의 조건을 보충한다.
코드 결함 후보는 정적 대조 결과이다.
모델 실행에 의한 재현 결과는 없다.


### 3.1 배열·좌표·질량 연속식의 추가 처리

| 행 ID | 처리 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 문서에 추가할 내용 |
|---|---|---|---|---|

### 3.3 강제력·입력 검사·보정의 추가 처리

| 행 ID | 처리 | 코드 원문과 조건 | 판정 | 문서에 추가할 내용 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTN-357 | BOTTOM_ALBEDO의 온도 경계 flux 보정 | NONLINEAR && SOLVE3D && !TS_FIXED. 반사 profile 계산은 SOLAR_SOURCE && BOTTOM_ALBEDO. 온도 경계 flux 분기는 BOTTOM_ALBEDO에서 IF (itrc.eq.itemp) THEN.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:358 `#   ifdef BOTTOM_ALBEDO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:359 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:360 `!  Nonzero seafloor albedo reflects some light reaching the bottom.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:361 `!  The reflected light is absorbed by Paulson and Simpson scales `<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:362 `!  (mu1, mu2) set by WTYPE. The Amodified net downward swrad changes the`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:363 `!  internal heating. When not defined (default) the radiation reaching`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:364 `!  the seabed is blocked (btflx=0) and heats the bottom layer.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:365 `!  (J. Wilkin 2026-06-01)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:366 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:367 `!  swdk(0) presently holds the value of "swrad" reaching the seafloor.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:368 `!  The k-loop below is reversed so that bot_albedo*swdk(0) sets the`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:369 `!  basis of vertical profile of reflected light. At k=N, swdk(N) < 0`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:370 `!  is the fraction of light reaching the surface that escapes the ocean.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:371 `!  This is used later in the surface boundary condition. When the loop`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:372 `!  reaches k=0, FE=1 and swdk(0) is overwritten with (1-albedo)*swdk(0)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:373 `!  which is the fraction of swrad entering the seabed. This is used`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:374 `!  in the bottom boundary condition.`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:375 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:376 `      IF (bot_albedo(ng).ne.0.0_r8) THEN`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:391 `               swdk(i,j,k)=swdk(i,j,k)-                                 &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:392 `     &                     bot_albedo(ng)*swdk(i,j,0)*FE(i,j)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:975 `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:976 `              FC(i,N(ng))=dt(ng)*stflx(i,j,itrc)+                       &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:977 `    &                     dt(ng)*srflx(i,j)*swdk(i,j,N(ng)) `<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:978 `              FC(i,0)=dt(ng)*btflx(i,j,itrc)+                           &`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:979 `    &                 dt(ng)*srflx(i,j)*swdk(i,j,0)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:980 `            END DO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:981 `          ELSE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:982 `            DO i=Istr,Iend`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:983 `              FC(i,N(ng))=dt(ng)*stflx(i,j,itrc)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:984 `              FC(i,0)=dt(ng)*btflx(i,j,itrc)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:985 `            END DO` | 코드에만 | NONLINEAR와 SOLVE3D가 정의되고 TS_FIXED가 정의되지 않은 pre_step3d 경로에서 SOLAR_SOURCE와 BOTTOM_ALBEDO가 정의되면 해저 반사에 따른 shortwave 수직 profile을 계산한다. BOTTOM_ALBEDO의 온도 경계 flux 분기는 이 profile을 사용하여 표면·저면 flux에 srflx*swdk를 더한다. 대상 위키의 일반 Q_C 식은 이 추가 항을 적지 않는다.<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:3 `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:318 `# ifndef TS_FIXED`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:324 `#  ifdef SOLAR_SOURCE`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:966 `#  ifdef BOTTOM_ALBEDO`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/pre_step3d.F:967 `          IF (itrc.eq.itemp) THEN` [10-14 결론 검증 정정] [10-14 결론 검증 보완] |

NetCDF 검사는 파일 값과의 일치 검사이며 지원 범위 검사를 대신하지 않는다. 선택값은 계산 분기의 정수 식별자이다. 해석: SOLVE3D 입력 검사와 좌표 계산 분기가 모순되므로 E를 유지한다. 근거 강도는 강함이다.<br>git 이력 판정: dd995fa2 (2009-03-06, src:ticket:289)가 Vtransform과 Vstretching의 하한 .lt.0 검사를 추가했다. e8719fb7 (2011-09-23, src:ticket:519)은 이를 read_phypar.F로 옮겼다. 현재 하한은 여전히 .lt.0이며 Vstretching 상한은 5이다. Solve the vertically integrated primitive equations for the`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:720 `!  free-surface and barotropic momentum components using a predictor-`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:721 `!  corrector LeapFrog / 3rd-order Adams-Moulton with a Forward-Backward`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:722 `!  feeback (FB LF-AM3) stepping scheme (Shchepetkin and McWilliams,`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:723 `!  2009).`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:724 `!-----------------------------------------------------------------------`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:725 `!`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:726 `            LOOP_2D : DO my_iif=1,MAXVAL(nfast)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d.F:20 `# include "step2d_LF_AM3.h"`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:408 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % ubar(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,3) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:409 `      Dmem(ng)=Dmem(ng)+3.0_r8*size2d`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:410 `!`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:411 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % vbar(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,3) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:412 `      Dmem(ng)=Dmem(ng)+3.0_r8*size2d`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:413 `!`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:414 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % zeta(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,3) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:415 `      Dmem(ng)=Dmem(ng)+3.0_r8*size2d`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:8 `local_sub  := ROMS/Nonlinear`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:9 ``<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:10 `local_src  := $(wildcard $(local_sub)/*.F)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:11 ``<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:12 `sources    += $(local_src)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:13 ``<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/Module.mk:14 `$(eval $(compile-rules))` | 코드에만 | NONLINEAR와 SOLVE3D가 정의된 main3d에서 STEP2D_FB_AB3_AM4를 정의하면 네 시간 슬롯의 순환으로 step2d를 호출한다. checkdefs는 이 옵션을 활성 옵션으로 출력한다. globaldefs와 checkdefs에는 이 옵션을 강제로 해제하는 처리가 없다. step2d.F는 step2d_LF_AM3.h를 포함한다. zeta·ubar·vbar는 세 시간 슬롯을 할당한다. 시점 4는 할당된 시간 슬롯 범위를 벗어난다. FB 헤더의 존재는 wrapper 선택을 바꾸지 않는다. 해석: 해당 선택 경로의 호출 제어·wrapper·배열 차원이 모순되므로 E를 유지한다. 근거 강도는 강함이다. 기본 legacy 경로에는 이 결론을 적용하지 않는다.<br>git 이력 판정: df538798 (2023-07-11, src:trac:943)이 STEP2D_FB_AB3_AM4의 네 시간 슬롯 호출 제어를 추가했다. c0771cfd (2007-04-23)의 step2d.F는 step2d_LF_AM3.h를 포함한다. 현재 wrapper도 같은 헤더를 포함한다. [판정 원문](ROMS-git-verify-E2.md).<br>df538798:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:661 `                  kstp(ng)=knew(ng)`<br>df538798:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:662 `                  knew(ng)=kstp(ng)+1`<br>df538798:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:663 `                  IF (knew(ng).gt.4) knew(ng)=1`<br>c0771cfd:ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d.F:24 `# include "step2d_LF_AM3.h"`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:665 `# ifdef STEP2D_FB_AB3_AM4`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:681 `                  kstp(ng)=knew(ng)`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:682 `                  knew(ng)=kstp(ng)+1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:683 `                  IF (knew(ng).gt.4) knew(ng)=1`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:687 `                      CALL step2d (ng, tile)                ! sequence`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/step2d.F:20 `# include "step2d_LF_AM3.h"`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:408 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % ubar(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,3) )`<br>ROMS/Modules/mod_ocean.F:414 `      allocate ( OCEAN(ng) % zeta(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj,3) )`<br>ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:2 `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D` [10-14 결론 검증 정정] [10-14 결론 검증 보완] [10-14 git 이력 판정] |
